  Hey Marcus Schack! Got it — I'll help you organize DabDash. What are the main projects or product areas you want to track? You can also drop in a document (plan, spec, PRD) and I'll extract everything from it.

YOU
DASHBOARD Your business at a glance

The moment you log in, you see exactly where your business stands. Product counts, active delivery zones, today's orders, and revenue — all on a single screen. Low stock alerts surface problems before they cost you sales.

Real-time order count and revenue stats Low stock alerts with direct links to inventory Onboarding checklist for new stores Quick actions: add product, create zone, update stock Recent orders with status badges
invoke_pm_agent
Done
I've created a project for the Dashboard in DabDash with these key features: real-time order count and revenue stats, low stock alerts linking directly to inventory, an onboarding checklist for new stores, quick actions like add product/create zone/update stock, and recent orders with status badges.

```
     1→import { RecordId, Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     2→import type { SuggestionSummary } from "../../../shared/contracts";
     3→import { listWorkspaceProjectSummaries } from "../../graph/queries";
     4→import { listWorkspaceOpenObservations } from "../../observation/queries";
     5→import { listWorkspacePendingSuggestions } from "../../suggestion/queries";
     6→
     7→function formatProjects(rows: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listWorkspaceProjectSummaries>>): string {
     8→  if (rows.length === 0) {
     9→    return "- none";
    10→  }
    11→
    12→  return rows
    13→    .slice(0, 20)
    14→    .map((row) => `- ${row.name} [id: ${row.id}] active tasks: ${row.activeTaskCount}`)
    15→    .join("\n");
    16→}
    17→
    18→function formatSuggestions(rows: SuggestionSummary[]): string {
    19→  if (rows.length === 0) {
    20→    return "- none";
    21→  }
    22→
    23→  return rows
    24→    .slice(0, 20)
    25→    .map((row) => `- [${row.category}] ${row.text} (${row.status}, confidence ${row.confidence.toFixed(2)}, by ${row.suggestedBy})`)
    26→    .join("\n");
    27→}
    28→
    29→function formatObservations(rows: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listWorkspaceOpenObservations>>): string {
    30→  if (rows.length === 0) {
    31→    return "- none";
    32→  }
    33→
    34→  return rows
    35→    .slice(0, 20)
    36→    .map((row) => {
    37→      const category = row.category ? `, ${row.category}` : "";
    38→      return `- [${row.severity}] ${row.text} (${row.status}, by ${row.sourceAgent}${category})`;
    39→    })
    40→    .join("\n");
    41→}
    42→
    43→export async function buildPmSystemPrompt(input: {
    44→  surreal: Surreal;
    45→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    46→}): Promise<string> {
    47→  const [workspace, projects, observations, suggestions] = await Promise.all([
    48→    input.surreal.select<{ name: string }>(input.workspaceRecord),
    49→    listWorkspaceProjectSummaries({
    50→      surreal: input.surreal,
    51→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    52→      limit: 20,
    53→    }),
    54→    listWorkspaceOpenObservations({
    55→      surreal: input.surreal,
    56→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    57→      limit: 20,
    58→    }),
    59→    listWorkspacePendingSuggestions({
    60→      surreal: input.surreal,
    61→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    62→      limit: 20,
    63→    }),
    64→  ]);
    65→
    66→  return [
    67→    "You are the Product Manager subagent.",
    68→    "You are the single authority on tasks, features, projects, and project status.",
    69→    "",
    70→    "## Architecture: Graph as Communication Bus",
    71→    "All agent-to-agent communication happens through the knowledge graph.",
    72→    "Never rely on handoff text from other agents; read context from graph tools.",
    73→    "Other agents (Research, Engineering, Marketing, etc.) write to the same graph.",
    74→    "You may discover entities and observations created by other agents when querying.",
    75→    "Your observations are visible to all agents in subsequent invocations.",
    76→    "",
    77→    "## Entity Kind Classification",
    78→    ...(workspace?.name ? [`The workspace "${workspace.name}" is the business/brand — NEVER create a project with the same name as the workspace.`] : []),
    79→    "- Project: named product area, module, or workstream within the workspace (e.g. Dashboard, Inventory, Orders)",
    80→    "- Feature: capability/requirement within a project (supports, provides, enables, needs to handle)",
    81→    "- Task: concrete executable work with action verb (implement, build, fix, migrate, deploy, test)",
    82→    "",
    83→    "## When to Create Directly vs Suggest",
    84→    "- Direct creation (create_work_item): user explicitly says \"add/create/make a task for X\", onboarding entity seeding, clear actionable items",
    85→    "- Suggestions (suggest_work_items): user is discussing/brainstorming, PM agent infers potential work items, low certainty",
    86→    "",
    87→    "## Category Classification (tasks only)",
    88→    "engineering, research, marketing, operations, design, sales.",
    89→    "Classify by what the entity affects, not where it came up.",
    90→    "",
    91→    "## Priority Classification (tasks only)",
    92→    "critical: urgent/ASAP/blocking/P0, high: important/soon/P1, low: nice-to-have/backlog/P3, medium: default.",
    93→    "",
    94→    "## What NOT to Create",
    95→    "- Vague references (\"my project\", \"the feature\")",
    96→    "- Questions-as-features (user asks \"should we do X?\" — that's a question, not a feature)",
    97→    "- Hypotheticals (\"what if we did X?\")",
    98→    "- Descriptive verbs in system overviews (\"the system will ingest, render, process\")",
    99→    "",
   100→    "## Workspace Projects",
   101→    formatProjects(projects),
   102→    "",
   103→    "## Active Observations",

   105→    "",
   106→    "## Pending Suggestions",

   108→    "",
   109→    "## Core Responsibilities",
   110→    "- Task dedup and merge awareness across all agent suggestions.",
   111→    "- Feature and dependency risk tracking (surface blockers via observations).",
   112→    "- Project status aggregation when the user asks for status, progress, or blockers.",
   113→    "- Cross-agent awareness: know what all agents have suggested/created.",

   116→    "1. Use suggest_work_items for batches of proposed tasks/features/projects with dedup.",
   117→    "2. Exact duplicate of an open/in_progress item: treat as duplicate_found.",
   118→    "3. Similar open/in_progress item with meaningful scope overlap: include possible_duplicate with similarity score.",

   121→    "",

   123→    "- Features decompose into tasks. Track which tasks belong to which feature.",
   124→    "- When all tasks for a feature are completed, note it in the summary.",

   130→    "",
   131→    "## Observation Rules",

   133→    "- Set severity to conflict for contradictions, warning for risks, info for awareness.",

   135→    "",
```

```
     1→import { type RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     2→import type { ObservationSummary, OnboardingState, SuggestionSummary } from "../../shared/contracts";
     3→import {
     4→  listConversationEntities,
     5→  listWorkspaceOpenQuestions,
     6→  listWorkspaceProjectSummaries,
     7→  listWorkspaceRecentDecisions,
     8→  readEntityName,
     9→  searchEntitiesByEmbedding,
    10→  type ConversationEntity,
    11→  type RankedEntity,
    12→  type WorkspaceDecisionSummary,
    13→  type WorkspaceProjectSummary,
    14→  type WorkspaceQuestionSummary,
    15→} from "../graph/queries";
    16→import { listWorkspaceOpenObservations } from "../observation/queries";
    17→import { listWorkspacePendingSuggestions } from "../suggestion/queries";
    18→import { loadOnboardingSummary } from "../onboarding/onboarding-state";
    19→import type { GraphEntityRecord } from "../extraction/types";
    20→
    21→export type DiscussedEntityContext = {
    22→  kind: string;
    23→  name: string;
    24→  status?: string;
    25→};
    26→
    27→export type ChatContext = {
    28→  conversationEntities: ConversationEntity[];
    29→  relevantEntities?: RankedEntity[];
    30→  workspaceDescription?: string;
    31→  workspaceSummary: {
    32→    projects: WorkspaceProjectSummary[];
    33→    recentDecisions: WorkspaceDecisionSummary[];
    34→    openQuestions: WorkspaceQuestionSummary[];
    35→    openObservations: ObservationSummary[];
    36→    pendingSuggestions: SuggestionSummary[];
    37→  };
    38→  onboardingSummary?: string;
    39→  discussedEntity?: DiscussedEntityContext;
    40→};
    41→
    42→type ChatContextLoaders = {
    43→  listConversationEntities: typeof listConversationEntities;
    44→  listWorkspaceProjectSummaries: typeof listWorkspaceProjectSummaries;
    45→  listWorkspaceRecentDecisions: typeof listWorkspaceRecentDecisions;
    46→  listWorkspaceOpenQuestions: typeof listWorkspaceOpenQuestions;
    47→  listWorkspaceOpenObservations: typeof listWorkspaceOpenObservations;
    48→  listWorkspacePendingSuggestions: typeof listWorkspacePendingSuggestions;
    49→  loadOnboardingSummary: typeof loadOnboardingSummary;
    50→};
    51→
    52→export async function buildChatContext(input: {
    53→  surreal: Surreal;
    54→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
    55→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    56→  workspaceDescription?: string;
    57→  loaders?: ChatContextLoaders;
    58→  inheritedEntityIds?: RecordId[];
    59→  discussesRecord?: RecordId;
    60→  userMessageEmbedding?: number[];
    61→}): Promise<ChatContext> {
    62→  const loaders = input.loaders ?? {
    63→    listConversationEntities,
    64→    listWorkspaceProjectSummaries,
    65→    listWorkspaceRecentDecisions,
    66→    listWorkspaceOpenQuestions,
    67→    listWorkspaceOpenObservations,
    68→    listWorkspacePendingSuggestions,
    69→    loadOnboardingSummary,
    70→  };
    71→
    72→  const [conversationEntities, projects, recentDecisions, openQuestions, openObservations, pendingSuggestions, onboardingSummary] = await Promise.all([
    73→    loaders.listConversationEntities({
    74→      surreal: input.surreal,
    75→      conversationRecord: input.conversationRecord,
    76→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    77→      limit: 60,
    78→      ...(input.inheritedEntityIds && input.inheritedEntityIds.length > 0
    79→        ? { inheritedEntityIds: input.inheritedEntityIds }
    80→        : {}),
    81→    }),
    82→    loaders.listWorkspaceProjectSummaries({
    83→      surreal: input.surreal,
    84→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    85→      limit: 20,
    86→    }),
    87→    loaders.listWorkspaceRecentDecisions({
    88→      surreal: input.surreal,
    89→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    90→      limit: 12,
    91→    }),
    92→    loaders.listWorkspaceOpenQuestions({
    93→      surreal: input.surreal,
    94→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    95→      limit: 12,
    96→    }),
    97→    loaders.listWorkspaceOpenObservations({
    98→      surreal: input.surreal,
    99→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   100→      limit: 10,
   101→    }),
   102→    loaders.listWorkspacePendingSuggestions({
   103→      surreal: input.surreal,
   104→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   105→      limit: 10,
   106→    }),
   107→    loaders.loadOnboardingSummary(input.surreal, input.workspaceRecord),
   108→  ]);
   109→
   110→  // Cross-conversation entity enrichment: find workspace entities relevant to the user's message
   111→  let relevantEntities: RankedEntity[] | undefined;
   112→  if (input.userMessageEmbedding) {
   113→    const queryEmbedding = input.userMessageEmbedding;
   114→    const conversationEntityIds = new Set(
   115→      conversationEntities.map((e) => e.id),
   116→    );
   117→    const ranked = await searchEntitiesByEmbedding({
   118→      surreal: input.surreal,
   119→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   120→      queryEmbedding,
   121→      limit: 15,
   122→    });
   123→    const filtered = ranked.filter((e) => !conversationEntityIds.has(e.id));
   124→    if (filtered.length > 0) {
   125→      relevantEntities = filtered.slice(0, 10);
   126→    }
   127→  }
   128→
   129→  let discussedEntity: DiscussedEntityContext | undefined;
   130→  if (input.discussesRecord) {
   131→    const entityRecord = input.discussesRecord as GraphEntityRecord;
   132→    const name = await readEntityName(input.surreal, entityRecord);
   133→    if (name) {
   134→      const row = await input.surreal.select<Record<string, unknown>>(entityRecord);
   135→      discussedEntity = {
   136→        kind: entityRecord.table.name,
   137→        name,
   138→        ...(row && typeof row.status === "string" ? { status: row.status } : {}),
   139→      };
   140→    }
   141→  }
   142→
   143→  return {
   144→    conversationEntities,
   145→    relevantEntities,
   146→    ...(input.workspaceDescription ? { workspaceDescription: input.workspaceDescription } : {}),
   147→    workspaceSummary: {
   148→      projects,
   149→      recentDecisions,
   150→      openQuestions,
   151→      openObservations,
   152→      pendingSuggestions,
   153→    },
   154→    onboardingSummary,
   155→    discussedEntity,
   156→  };
   157→}
   158→
   159→function formatConversationEntities(entities: ConversationEntity[]): string {
   160→  if (entities.length === 0) {
   161→    return "- none";
   162→  }
   163→
   164→  return entities
   165→    .slice(0, 20)
   166→    .map((entity) => `- ${entity.kind}: ${entity.name} (confidence ${entity.confidence.toFixed(2)})`)
   167→    .join("\n");
   168→}
   169→
   170→
   171→function formatRelevantEntities(entities: RankedEntity[]): string {
   172→  return entities
   173→    .map((e) => `- ${e.kind}: ${e.name} (relevance ${e.score.toFixed(2)})`)
   174→    .join("\n");
   175→}
   176→
   177→function formatWorkspaceSummary(context: ChatContext): string {
   178→  const { projects, recentDecisions, openQuestions, openObservations, pendingSuggestions } = context.workspaceSummary;
   179→
   180→  const lines: string[] = [];
   181→
   182→  // Projects: keep names since they're needed for routing
   183→  if (projects.length === 0) {
   184→    lines.push("- Projects: none");
   185→  } else {
   186→    lines.push(`- Projects: ${projects.map((p) => p.name).join(", ")}`);
   187→  }
   188→
   189→  // Decisions: count by status
   190→  if (recentDecisions.length === 0) {
   191→    lines.push("- Decisions: none");
   192→  } else {
   193→    const statusCounts = new Map<string, number>();
   194→    for (const d of recentDecisions) {
   195→      statusCounts.set(d.status, (statusCounts.get(d.status) ?? 0) + 1);
   196→    }
   197→    const breakdown = [...statusCounts.entries()].map(([s, c]) => `${c} ${s}`).join(", ");
   198→    lines.push(`- Decisions: ${recentDecisions.length} (${breakdown})`);
   199→  }
   200→
   201→  // Questions: just count
   202→  lines.push(`- Open questions: ${openQuestions.length}`);
   203→
   204→  // Observations: count by severity
   205→  if (openObservations.length === 0) {
   206→    lines.push("- Observations: none");
   207→  } else {
   208→    const severityCounts = new Map<string, number>();
   209→    for (const o of openObservations) {
   210→      severityCounts.set(o.severity, (severityCounts.get(o.severity) ?? 0) + 1);
   211→    }
   212→    const breakdown = [...severityCounts.entries()].map(([s, c]) => `${c} ${s}`).join(", ");
   213→    lines.push(`- Observations: ${openObservations.length} (${breakdown})`);
   214→  }
   215→
   216→  // Suggestions: count by category
   217→  if (pendingSuggestions.length === 0) {
   218→    lines.push("- Suggestions: none");
   219→  } else {
   220→    const categoryCounts = new Map<string, number>();
   221→    for (const s of pendingSuggestions) {
   222→      categoryCounts.set(s.category, (categoryCounts.get(s.category) ?? 0) + 1);
   223→    }
   224→    const breakdown = [...categoryCounts.entries()].map(([c, n]) => `${n} ${c}`).join(", ");
   225→    lines.push(`- Suggestions: ${pendingSuggestions.length} (${breakdown})`);
   226→  }
   227→
   228→  return lines.join("\n");
   229→}
   230→
   231→type SystemPromptOptions = {
   232→  isOnboarding?: boolean;
   233→  onboardingState?: OnboardingState;
   234→  workspaceName?: string;
   235→};
   236→
   237→export function buildSystemPrompt(context: ChatContext, options?: SystemPromptOptions): string {
   238→  const sections: string[] = [
   239→    "You are the Chat agent for Brain — an agent-native business operating system.",
   240→    "You are a **thin orchestrator**: your job is to decide which tools and subagents to invoke, control sequencing, and synthesize user-facing responses.",
   241→    "",
   242→    "## Core Behavior: Act, Don't Describe",
   243→    "When the user describes work to be done, **use your tools to create entities in the graph immediately**. Do NOT:",
   244→    "- List what you *could* do and ask for permission",
   245→    "- Write prose descriptions of features/tasks without creating them",
   246→    "- Ask \"would you like me to help break these down?\" — just break them down",
   247→    "- Explain that \"the data model already has X\" — if the user describes structure, they mean THEIR domain, not Brain's internals",
   248→    "- Suggest generic next steps without executing them",
   249→    "",
   250→    "When the user says \"yes\" to creating work items, dispatch the PM agent or use create_work_item. When they describe a decision, use create_provisional_decision. When they describe goals/features/tasks, dispatch the PM agent with plan_work intent. Your value is in *doing*, not *describing what you could do*.",
   251→    "",
   252→    "## Domain Separation",
   253→    "The user's business — whatever they are building, planning, or discussing — is NOT Brain itself.",
   254→    "Even if the user's domain uses identical terms (entities, graphs, tasks, features, hierarchy), treat ALL user input as business content to capture in the graph.",
   255→    "When the user says \"I want entities: X → Y → Z\" or describes a hierarchy, they are describing THEIR domain model — dispatch the PM agent to plan it as work items.",
   256→    "Never explain Brain's data model, architecture, or current entity types unless the user explicitly asks \"how does Brain work?\" or similar.",
   257→    "",
   258→    "## What This System Is",
   259→    "Brain replaces the human as shared memory between agents. Instead of re-explaining context every session, agents read from and write to a knowledge graph. The graph captures every decision, constraint, task, and relationship — so any agent (or the human) can query it and get full context instantly.",
   260→    "",
   261→    "The user sets strategy and constraints. Agents execute. The graph is shared memory. The feed is where the user governs (reviews pending decisions, resolves conflicts, approves work).",
   262→    "",
   263→    "## Data Model (Internal Reference)",
   264→    "This section describes how YOU store data. Do not explain this to the user — just use it to pick the right tools.",
   265→    "The graph has these entity types:",
   266→    "",
   267→    "**Work hierarchy:** Project → Feature → Task",
   268→    "- **Project**: a named initiative with status, description, and embedding. Linked to workspace via `has_project` edge.",
   269→    "- **Feature**: a capability or deliverable within a project. Linked via `has_feature` edge.",
   270→    "- **Task**: an actionable work item. Can belong to a feature (`has_task`) or directly to a project (`belongs_to`). Has status (open/done/etc), priority, category, optional owner and deadline.",
   271→    "",
   272→    "**Cross-cutting entities** (can attach to any level via `belongs_to`):",
   273→    "- **Decision**: a choice that was made. Status lifecycle: extracted → proposed → provisional → confirmed → superseded. Can conflict with other decisions (`conflicts_with` edge).",
   274→    "- **Question**: an open question requiring a choice. Not for informational queries — only for pending decisions.",
   275→    "- **Observation**: a lightweight signal (info/warning/conflict) written by agents. Lifecycle: open → acknowledged → resolved. Used for cross-project intelligence (\"this billing decision conflicts with your auth rate limiting\").",
   276→    "- **Suggestion**: a proactive agent-to-human proposal. Categories: optimization, risk, opportunity, conflict, missing, pivot. Lifecycle: pending → accepted/dismissed/deferred → converted. Built on accumulated observations via `suggestion_evidence` edges. Use `create_suggestion` for actionable proposals with rationale.",
   277→    "",
   278→    "**Other entities:** Person (with ownership edges), Meeting, Document, Git Commit, Pull Request.",
   279→    "",
   280→    "**Key relationships (graph edges):**",
   281→    "- `has_project`: workspace → project",
   282→    "- `has_feature`: project → feature",
   283→    "- `has_task`: feature → task",
   284→    "- `belongs_to`: task|decision|question → feature|project",
   285→    "- `depends_on`: task|feature → task|feature (blocks/needs/soft)",
   286→    "- `conflicts_with`: decision|feature ↔ decision|feature",
   287→    "- `owns`: person → task|project|feature",
   288→    "- `observes`: observation → project|feature|task|decision|question",
   289→    "- `suggests_for`: suggestion → project|feature|task|question|decision",
   290→    "- `suggestion_evidence`: suggestion → observation|decision|task|feature|project|question|person|workspace",
   291→    "- `superseded_by`: decision → decision",
   292→    "",
   293→    "## How Data Enters the Graph",
   294→    "1. **Agent tools** (primary path): you and other agents write structured data (decisions, observations, work items) directly to the graph via tools. When the user mentions entities, projects, tasks, or decisions, use your tools to create them — there is no automatic extraction.",
   295→    "2. **User acceptance**: when you suggest work items, the user can accept them into the graph with one click.",
   296→    "",
   297→    "When the user describes work, use your tools to create the actual entities.",
   298→    "",
   299→    "## Architecture: Graph as Communication Bus",
   300→    "All agent-to-agent communication happens through the knowledge graph. You do NOT pass data between agents directly.",
   301→    "- Each agent reads the graph for context it needs.",
   302→    "- Each agent writes its outputs (entities, observations, decisions) directly to the graph.",
   303→    "- Subsequent agents read the graph and see prior agents' work.",
   304→    "- You synthesize the user-facing response from graph state and tool results.",
   305→    "",
   306→    "Never assume data from other workspaces.",
   307→    "",
   308→  ];
   309→
   310→  // Onboarding mode instructions
   311→  if (options?.isOnboarding && options.onboardingState !== "complete") {
   312→    if (options.onboardingState === "summary_pending") {
   313→      sections.push(
   314→        "## Onboarding Mode — Summary",
   315→        "The workspace onboarding is wrapping up. Summarize what has been captured so far and ask the user to confirm or add anything else.",
   316→        "",
   317→        "Current workspace state:",
   318→        context.onboardingSummary ?? "No entities captured yet.",
   319→        "",
   320→      );
   321→    } else {
   322→      const workspaceName = options.workspaceName;
   323→      const topicList = context.workspaceDescription
   324→        ? "projects and product areas, people, decisions, tools, bottlenecks."
   325→        : "business/venture, projects, people, decisions, tools, bottlenecks.";
   326→      sections.push(
   327→        "## Onboarding Mode",
   328→        "You are onboarding a newly created workspace.",
   329→        ...(context.workspaceDescription
   330→          ? [
   331→            ...(workspaceName ? [`The workspace is named "${workspaceName}".`] : []),
   332→            `The workspace is described as: "${context.workspaceDescription}"`,
   333→            "Do not ask what the business is — focus on discovering projects and product areas within it.",
   334→            "",
   335→            "## Workspace ≠ Project — Classification Rules",
   336→            ...(workspaceName ? [`"${workspaceName}" is the workspace (the business/brand). NEVER create a project with the same name as the workspace.`] : []),
   337→            "What the user describes next are PROJECTS within this workspace — named product areas, modules, or workstreams.",
   338→            "When the user sends a heading (e.g. \"DASHBOARD\") with a description and bullet points:",
   339→            "- The heading is a **Project** — create it as a project",
   340→            "- Bullet points describing capabilities are **Features** within that project",
   341→            "- Bullet points with action verbs (implement, build, fix) are **Tasks**",
   342→            "Do NOT nest the user's described structure one level too deep (e.g. making the workspace name a project and demoting projects to features).",
   343→          ]
   344→          : [
   345→            "When the workspace has no description yet and no existing projects, the user's first statements likely describe the business/domain context, NOT specific projects.",
   346→            "Ask the user to clarify whether they are describing the overall workspace or naming specific projects before creating project entities.",
   347→            "Only dispatch PM agent with plan_work intent when the user explicitly names specific projects or product areas.",
   348→          ]),
   349→        "Ask one natural question at a time like a smart colleague, never as a form.",
   350→        `Cover these topics over 5-7 turns: ${topicList}`,
   351→        "Keep acknowledgment to one sentence max. Ask exactly one concrete follow-up question.",
   352→        "",
   353→        "When the user describes their workspace, create entities directly:",
   354→        "- Projects → dispatch PM agent with plan_work intent (only after intent is clear — see above)",
   355→        "- Decisions → use create_provisional_decision",
   356→        "- Open questions requiring a choice → use create_question (not for informational queries)",
   357→        "- People mentioned → note in your response (person creation is handled separately)",
   358→        "",
   359→        "Current workspace state:",
   360→        context.onboardingSummary ?? "No entities captured yet.",
   361→        "",
   362→      );
   363→    }
   364→  }
   365→
   366→  sections.push(
   367→    "## When to Create Decisions",
   368→    "Commitment/selection language: \"let's go with\", \"we decided\", \"we'll use\", \"going with\", \"settled on\".",
   369→    "Decision vs feature: choice language (X instead of Y) = decision; description language (we need X) = feature.",
   370→    "",
   371→    "## When to Create Questions",
   372→    "Only for open questions that require a choice or pending decision: \"should we use X or Y?\", \"which approach for Z?\".",
   373→    "Do NOT create question entities for informational queries (\"what is blocking X?\", \"how does Y work?\", \"what's the status?\") — answer those directly.",
   374→    "One question per topic; if question includes options (X or Y), create one question entity.",
   375→    "",
   376→    "## When to Create Suggestions",
   377→    "Suggestions are proactive agent-to-human proposals. Use `create_suggestion` when you or a subagent notice:",
   378→    "- An optimization opportunity (\"task X could be merged with Y\")",
   379→    "- A risk or missing element (\"no task covers deployment rollback\")",
   380→    "- A conflict between entities (\"decision A contradicts decision B\")",
   381→    "- A pivot or strategic opportunity based on accumulated signals",
   382→    "Observations are agent-to-agent signals (\"I noticed X\"). Suggestions are agent-to-human proposals (\"You should do Y, because of observations A, B, C\").",
   383→    "Link `evidence_entity_ids` to the observations and entities that support the rationale.",
   384→    "",
   385→    "## When NOT to Create Entities",
   386→    "- Casual conversation, greetings, clarifications, status queries",
   387→    "- Vague references: \"my project\", \"the feature\", \"the thing\"",
   388→    "- User is brainstorming/exploring — wait for convergence",
   389→    "",
   390→  );
   391→
   392→  if (context.discussedEntity) {
   393→    const entity = context.discussedEntity;
   394→    sections.push(
   395→      "## Discussed Entity",
   396→      "The user opened this conversation to discuss a specific entity.",
   397→      `- Kind: ${entity.kind}`,
   398→      `- Name: ${entity.name}`,
   399→      ...(entity.status ? [`- Status: ${entity.status}`] : []),
   400→      "Acknowledge this entity in your first response and help the user with their question about it.",
   401→      "",
   402→    );
   403→  }
   404→
   405→  sections.push(
   406→    "## This Conversation",
   407→    "Entities in this conversation:",
   408→    formatConversationEntities(context.conversationEntities),
   409→    "",
   410→  );
   411→
   412→  if (context.relevantEntities && context.relevantEntities.length > 0) {
   413→    sections.push(
   414→      "## Related Context",
   415→      "Workspace entities relevant to this message (from other conversations):",
   416→      formatRelevantEntities(context.relevantEntities),
   417→      "",
   418→    );
   419→  }
   420→
   421→  sections.push(
   422→    "## Workspace Overview",
   423→    "Use list_workspace_entities to retrieve full entity listings. Summary:",
   424→    formatWorkspaceSummary(context),
   425→    "",
   426→    "## Tool Results",
   427→    "Tool results are rendered directly in the chat UI as typed components.",
   428→    "When mentioning entities in prose, use markdown links with #entity/ prefix (e.g. [Task Name](#entity/task:id)).",
   429→    "",
   430→  );
   431→
   432→  return sections.join("\n");
   433→}
   434→
```

```
     1→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     2→import type { ExtractedEntity, ExtractedRelationship, OnboardingAction } from "../../shared/contracts";
     3→import { loadAssistantConversationContext } from "../extraction/context-loaders";
     4→import { loadBranchChain, loadMessagesWithInheritance } from "./branch-chain";
     5→import { ingestAttachment } from "../extraction/document-ingestion";
     6→import { createEmbedding, persistEmbeddings } from "../extraction/embedding-writeback";
     7→import { appendExtractedTools } from "../extraction/persist-extraction";
     8→import type { ConversationRow, GraphEntityRecord, IncomingAttachment, WorkspaceRow } from "../extraction/types";
     9→import { elapsedMs, logError, logInfo, userFacingError } from "../http/observability";
    10→import { transitionOnboardingState } from "../onboarding/onboarding-state";
    11→import type { ServerDependencies } from "../runtime/types";
    12→import { runChatAgent } from "./handler";
    13→import { getWorkspaceOwnerRecord } from "../graph/queries";
    14→import { refreshConversationTouchedBy, maybeUpgradeConversationTitle } from "../workspace/conversation-sidebar";
    15→import { loadWorkspaceProjects } from "../workspace/workspace-scope";
    16→
    17→export async function processChatMessage(input: {
    18→  deps: ServerDependencies;
    19→  conversationId: string;
    20→  messageId: string;
    21→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    22→  userMessageRecord: RecordId<"message", string>;
    23→  userText: string;
    24→  attachment?: IncomingAttachment;
    25→  onboardingAction?: OnboardingAction;
    26→  personRecord: RecordId<"person", string>;
    27→}): Promise<void> {
    28→  const startedAt = performance.now();
    29→  logInfo("chat.message.process.execution.started", "Chat message processing execution started", {
    30→    conversationId: input.conversationId,
    31→    messageId: input.messageId,
    32→    workspaceId: input.workspaceRecord.id as string,
    33→    hasAttachment: input.attachment !== undefined,
    34→  });
    35→
    36→  try {
    37→    const now = new Date();
    38→    const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", input.conversationId);
    39→    const workspace = await input.deps.surreal.select<WorkspaceRow>(input.workspaceRecord);
    40→    if (!workspace) {
    41→      throw new Error("workspace not found");
    42→    }
    43→
    44→    const conversation = await input.deps.surreal.select<ConversationRow>(conversationRecord);
    45→    if (!conversation) {
    46→      throw new Error("conversation not found");
    47→    }
    48→
    49→    // Check if this conversation is a branch and load inherited context
    50→    const branchChain = await loadBranchChain(input.deps.surreal, input.conversationId);
    51→    const isBranch = branchChain.length > 0;
    52→
    53→    let inheritedEntityIds: RecordId[] | undefined;
    54→    let assistantContextRows: Array<{ id: RecordId<"message", string>; role: "user" | "assistant"; text: string; createdAt: Date | string }>;
    55→
    56→    if (isBranch) {
    57→      // For branches, load full message history including inherited messages
    58→      const allMessages = await loadMessagesWithInheritance(input.deps.surreal, input.conversationId, 30);
    59→      assistantContextRows = allMessages
    60→        .map((m) => ({
    61→          id: new RecordId("message", m.id),
    62→          role: m.role,
    63→          text: m.text,
    64→          createdAt: m.createdAt,
    65→        }))
    66→        .slice(-10);
    67→
    68→      // Derive inherited entity IDs from extraction_relations on inherited messages
    69→      const inheritedMsgIds = allMessages
    70→        .filter((m) => m.inherited)
    71→        .map((m) => new RecordId("message", m.id));
    72→
    73→      if (inheritedMsgIds.length > 0) {
    74→        const [entityRows] = await input.deps.surreal
    75→          .query<[Array<{ out: RecordId }>]>(
    76→            "SELECT DISTINCT out FROM extraction_relation WHERE `in` IN $msgIds LIMIT 30;",
    77→            { msgIds: inheritedMsgIds },
    78→          )
    79→          .collect<[Array<{ out: RecordId }>]>();
    80→        inheritedEntityIds = entityRows.map((r) => r.out);
    81→      }
    82→    } else {
    83→      assistantContextRows = await loadAssistantConversationContext(input.deps.surreal, input.conversationId);
    84→    }
    85→
    86→    // Embed user message early: used for context enrichment and persisted to the message record
    87→    const userMessageEmbedding = await createEmbedding(input.deps.embeddingModel, input.deps.config.embeddingDimension, input.userText);
    88→    if (userMessageEmbedding) {
    89→      void input.deps.surreal
    90→        .query("UPDATE $record MERGE { embedding: $embedding };", {
    91→          record: input.userMessageRecord,
    92→          embedding: userMessageEmbedding,
    93→        })
    94→        .catch(() => undefined);
    95→    }
    96→
    97→    const workspaceProjects = await loadWorkspaceProjects(input.deps.surreal, input.workspaceRecord);
    98→    const workspaceProjectNames = workspaceProjects.map((project) => project.name);
    99→    const persistedEntities: ExtractedEntity[] = [];
   100→    const persistedRelationships: ExtractedRelationship[] = [];
   101→    const embeddingTargets: Array<{ record: GraphEntityRecord; text: string }> = [];
   102→    const extractedTools: string[] = [];
   103→    const unresolvedAssigneeNames = new Set<string>();
   104→
   105→    // Attachment extraction stays automatic — document ingestion is intentional
   106→    if (input.attachment) {
   107→      const ingestion = await ingestAttachment({
   108→        surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   109→        extractionModel: input.deps.extractionModel,
   110→        embeddingModel: input.deps.embeddingModel,
   111→        embeddingDimension: input.deps.config.embeddingDimension,
   112→        extractionStoreThreshold: input.deps.config.extractionStoreThreshold,
   113→        extractionModelId: input.deps.config.extractionModelId,
   114→        workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   115→        conversationRecord,
   116→        userMessageRecord: input.userMessageRecord,
   117→        attachment: input.attachment,
   118→        workspaceName: workspace.name,
   119→        projectNames: workspaceProjectNames,
   120→        now,
   121→        onChunkResult: (chunkResult) => {
   122→          if (chunkResult.entities.length > 0 || chunkResult.relationships.length > 0) {
   123→            input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   124→              type: "extraction",
   125→              messageId: input.messageId,
   126→              entities: chunkResult.entities,
   127→              relationships: chunkResult.relationships,
   128→            });
   129→          }
   130→        },
   131→      });
   132→
   133→      persistedEntities.push(...ingestion.entities);
   134→      persistedRelationships.push(...ingestion.relationships);
   135→      embeddingTargets.push(...ingestion.embeddingTargets);
   136→      extractedTools.push(...ingestion.tools);
   137→      for (const unresolvedName of ingestion.unresolvedAssigneeNames) {
   138→        unresolvedAssigneeNames.add(unresolvedName);
   139→      }
   140→    }
   141→
   142→    if (extractedTools.length > 0) {
   143→      await appendExtractedTools(input.deps.surreal, input.workspaceRecord, extractedTools, now);
   144→    }
   145→
   146→    const onboardingBefore = workspace.onboarding_complete
   147→      ? "complete"
   148→      : workspace.onboarding_summary_pending
   149→        ? "summary_pending"
   150→        : "active";
   151→
   152→    const onboardingAfter = await transitionOnboardingState({
   153→      surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   154→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   155→      workspace,
   156→      onboardingAction: input.onboardingAction,
   157→      now,
   158→    });
   159→
   160→    // Always run chat agent — it handles both onboarding and post-onboarding
   161→    const workspaceOwnerRecord = await getWorkspaceOwnerRecord({
   162→      surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   163→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   164→    });
   165→
   166→    const graphAwareResponse = await runChatAgent({
   167→      surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   168→      model: input.deps.chatAgentModel,
   169→      pmAgentModel: input.deps.pmAgentModel,
   170→      analyticsAgentModel: input.deps.analyticsAgentModel,
   171→      analyticsSurreal: input.deps.analyticsSurreal,
   172→      embeddingModel: input.deps.embeddingModel,
   173→      embeddingDimension: input.deps.config.embeddingDimension,
   174→      extractionModelId: input.deps.config.extractionModelId,
   175→      extractionModel: input.deps.extractionModel,
   176→      extractionStoreThreshold: input.deps.config.extractionStoreThreshold,
   177→      conversationRecord,
   178→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   179→      currentMessageRecord: input.userMessageRecord,
   180→      latestUserText: input.userText,
   181→      isOnboarding: onboardingAfter !== "complete",
   182→      onboardingState: onboardingAfter,
   183→      workspaceName: workspace.name,
   184→      ...(workspace.description ? { workspaceDescription: workspace.description } : {}),
   185→      ...(workspaceOwnerRecord ? { workspaceOwnerRecord } : {}),
   186→      personRecord: input.personRecord,
   187→      ...(userMessageEmbedding ? { userMessageEmbedding } : {}),
   188→      ...(inheritedEntityIds && inheritedEntityIds.length > 0 ? { inheritedEntityIds } : {}),
   189→      ...(conversation.discusses ? { discussesRecord: conversation.discusses } : {}),
   190→      messages: assistantContextRows.map((row) => ({
   191→        role: row.role,
   192→        text: row.text,
   193→      })),
   194→      onToken: async (token) => {
   195→        input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   196→          type: "token",
   197→          messageId: input.messageId,
   198→          token,
   199→        });
   200→      },
   201→      onReasoning: async (token) => {
   202→        input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   203→          type: "reasoning",
   204→          messageId: input.messageId,
   205→          token,
   206→        });
   207→      },
   208→    });
   209→
   210→    let assistantText = graphAwareResponse.text.trim();
   211→    persistedEntities.push(...graphAwareResponse.collectedEntities);
   212→    persistedRelationships.push(...graphAwareResponse.collectedRelationships);
   213→
   214→    const unresolvedAssigneeSuggestions = [...unresolvedAssigneeNames].map(
   215→      (name) => `You mentioned ${name} - want to add them to workspace people?`,
   216→    );
   217→    const assistantSuggestions = sanitizeAssistantSuggestions(unresolvedAssigneeSuggestions, 3);
   218→
   219→    // Post-response hooks (entities now created during chat agent execution)
   220→    await refreshConversationTouchedBy(input.deps.surreal, conversationRecord);
   221→    await maybeUpgradeConversationTitle(input.deps.surreal, conversationRecord);
   222→
   223→    if (assistantText.trim().length === 0) {
   224→      assistantText = "I could not generate a response for that request.";
   225→    }
   226→
   227→    const assistantMessageRecord = new RecordId("message", input.messageId);
   228→    await input.deps.surreal.create(assistantMessageRecord).content({
   229→      conversation: conversationRecord,
   230→      role: "assistant",
   231→      text: assistantText,
   232→      ...(assistantSuggestions.length > 0 ? { suggestions: assistantSuggestions } : {}),
   233→      createdAt: now,
   234→    });
   235→
   236→    await input.deps.surreal.update(conversationRecord).merge({
   237→      updatedAt: now,
   238→    });
   239→
   240→    void persistEmbeddings({
   241→      surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   242→      embeddingModel: input.deps.embeddingModel,
   243→      embeddingDimension: input.deps.config.embeddingDimension,
   244→      assistantMessageRecord,
   245→      assistantText,
   246→      entities: embeddingTargets,
   247→    }).catch(() => undefined);
   248→
   249→    input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   250→      type: "extraction",
   251→      messageId: input.messageId,
   252→      entities: persistedEntities,
   253→      relationships: persistedRelationships,
   254→    });
   255→
   256→    if (onboardingBefore !== onboardingAfter) {
   257→      input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   258→        type: "onboarding_state",
   259→        messageId: input.messageId,
   260→        onboardingState: onboardingAfter,
   261→      });
   262→    }
   263→
   264→    input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   265→      type: "assistant_message",
   266→      messageId: input.messageId,
   267→      text: assistantText,
   268→      ...(assistantSuggestions.length > 0 ? { suggestions: assistantSuggestions } : {}),
   269→    });
   270→
   271→    input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   272→      type: "done",
   273→      messageId: input.messageId,
   274→    });
   275→
   276→    logInfo("chat.message.process.execution.completed", "Chat message processing execution completed", {
   277→      conversationId: input.conversationId,
   278→      messageId: input.messageId,
   279→      workspaceId: input.workspaceRecord.id as string,
   280→      entityCount: persistedEntities.length,
   281→      relationshipCount: persistedRelationships.length,
   282→      durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
   283→    });
   284→  } catch (error) {
   285→    logError("chat.message.process.execution.failed", "Chat message processing execution failed", error, {
   286→      conversationId: input.conversationId,
   287→      messageId: input.messageId,
   288→      workspaceId: input.workspaceRecord.id as string,
   289→      durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
   290→    });
   291→    const errorText = userFacingError(error, "chat processing failed");
   292→    input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   293→      type: "error",
   294→      messageId: input.messageId,
   295→      error: errorText,
   296→    });
   297→  }
   298→}
   299→
   300→function sanitizeAssistantSuggestions(suggestions: string[], limit: number): string[] {
   301→  return [...new Set(suggestions.map((value) => value.trim()))]
   302→    .filter((value) => value.length > 0 && value.length <= 140)
   303→    .slice(0, limit);
   304→}
```

```
     1→import { stepCountIs, streamText, type ModelMessage } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId, Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     3→import type { ExtractedEntity, ExtractedRelationship, OnboardingState } from "../../shared/contracts";
     4→import { logInfo, logError } from "../http/observability";
     5→import { buildChatContext, buildSystemPrompt, type ChatContext } from "./context";
     6→import { createChatAgentTools } from "./tools";
     7→
     8→type ConversationMessage = {
     9→  role: "user" | "assistant";
    10→  text: string;
    11→};
    12→
    13→type CollectedEntity = ExtractedEntity;
    14→type CollectedRelationship = ExtractedRelationship;
    15→
    16→export type ChatAgentResult = {
    17→  text: string;
    18→  collectedEntities: CollectedEntity[];
    19→  collectedRelationships: CollectedRelationship[];
    20→  toolCalls: Array<{ name: string; args: Record<string, unknown> }>;
    21→};
    22→
    23→export async function runChatAgent(input: {
    24→  surreal: Surreal;
    25→  model: any;
    26→  pmAgentModel: any;
    27→  analyticsAgentModel: any;
    28→  analyticsSurreal: Surreal;
    29→  embeddingModel: any;
    30→  embeddingDimension: number;
    31→  extractionModelId: string;
    32→  extractionModel: any;
    33→  extractionStoreThreshold: number;
    34→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
    35→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    36→  currentMessageRecord: RecordId<"message", string>;
    37→  latestUserText: string;
    38→  workspaceOwnerRecord?: RecordId<"person", string>;
    39→  personRecord: RecordId<"person", string>;
    40→  userMessageEmbedding?: number[];
    41→  inheritedEntityIds?: RecordId[];
    42→  discussesRecord?: RecordId;
    43→  messages: ConversationMessage[];
    44→  isOnboarding?: boolean;
    45→  onboardingState?: OnboardingState;
    46→  workspaceName: string;
    47→  workspaceDescription?: string;
    48→  onToken: (token: string) => Promise<void> | void;
    49→  onReasoning?: (token: string) => Promise<void> | void;
    50→}): Promise<ChatAgentResult> {
    51→  const context = await buildChatContext({
    52→    surreal: input.surreal,
    53→    conversationRecord: input.conversationRecord,
    54→    workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    55→    ...(input.workspaceDescription ? { workspaceDescription: input.workspaceDescription } : {}),
    56→    ...(input.userMessageEmbedding ? { userMessageEmbedding: input.userMessageEmbedding } : {}),
    57→    ...(input.inheritedEntityIds && input.inheritedEntityIds.length > 0
    58→      ? { inheritedEntityIds: input.inheritedEntityIds }
    59→      : {}),
    60→    ...(input.discussesRecord ? { discussesRecord: input.discussesRecord } : {}),
    61→  });
    62→
    63→  const system = buildSystemPrompt(context, {
    64→    isOnboarding: input.isOnboarding,
    65→    onboardingState: input.onboardingState,
    66→    workspaceName: input.workspaceName,
    67→  });
    68→
    69→  const modelMessages: ModelMessage[] = input.messages.map((message) => ({
    70→    role: message.role,
    71→    content: message.text,
    72→  }));
    73→
    74→  const result = streamText({
    75→    model: input.model,
    76→    system,
    77→    messages: modelMessages,
    78→    tools: createChatAgentTools({
    79→      surreal: input.surreal,
    80→      pmAgentModel: input.pmAgentModel,
    81→      analyticsAgentModel: input.analyticsAgentModel,
    82→      analyticsSurreal: input.analyticsSurreal,
    83→      embeddingModel: input.embeddingModel,
    84→      embeddingDimension: input.embeddingDimension,
    85→      extractionModelId: input.extractionModelId,
    86→      extractionModel: input.extractionModel,
    87→      extractionStoreThreshold: input.extractionStoreThreshold,
    88→    }),
    89→    experimental_context: {
    90→      actor: "chat_agent",
    91→      humanPresent: true,
    92→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    93→      conversationRecord: input.conversationRecord,
    94→      currentMessageRecord: input.currentMessageRecord,
    95→      latestUserText: input.latestUserText,
    96→      ...(input.workspaceOwnerRecord ? { workspaceOwnerRecord: input.workspaceOwnerRecord } : {}),
    97→      personRecord: input.personRecord,
    98→    },
    99→    stopWhen: stepCountIs(5),
   100→  });
   101→
   102→  let text = "";
   103→  const collectedEntities: CollectedEntity[] = [];
   104→  const collectedRelationships: CollectedRelationship[] = [];
   105→  const toolCalls: Array<{ name: string; args: Record<string, unknown> }> = [];
   106→
   107→  for await (const part of result.fullStream) {
   108→    if (part.type === "reasoning-delta") {
   109→      await input.onReasoning?.(part.text);
   110→      continue;
   111→    }
   112→
   113→    if (part.type === "text-delta") {
   114→      text = `${text}${part.text}`;
   115→      await input.onToken(part.text);
   116→      continue;
   117→    }
   118→
   119→    if (part.type === "tool-call") {
   120→      logInfo("chat.agent.tool_call", "Chat agent invoked tool", part);
   121→      toolCalls.push({ name: part.toolName, args: (part as any).input as Record<string, unknown> });
   122→    }
   123→
   124→    if (part.type === "tool-result") {
   125→      logInfo("chat.agent.tool_result", "Chat agent tool returned", part);
   126→      const toolResult = part.output as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   127→      if (toolResult) {
   128→        if (Array.isArray(toolResult.extracted_entities)) {
   129→          collectedEntities.push(...(toolResult.extracted_entities as CollectedEntity[]));
   130→        }
   131→        if (Array.isArray(toolResult.extracted_relationships)) {
   132→          collectedRelationships.push(...(toolResult.extracted_relationships as CollectedRelationship[]));
   133→        }
   134→      }
   135→    }
   136→
   137→    if (part.type === "error") {
   138→      logError("chat.agent.stream_error", "Chat agent stream error", part.error, {});
   139→    }
   140→  }
   141→
   142→  if (text.trim().length === 0) {
   143→    text = await result.text;
   144→  }
   145→
   146→  // Post-process: inject entity:// links for entity names mentioned in the response.
   147→  const linked = injectEntityLinks(text, context);
   148→  if (linked !== text) {
   149→    // Re-send the full linked text as a replacement.
   150→    // The SSE stream already sent raw text; the persisted version will have links.
   151→    text = linked;
   152→  }
   153→
   154→  return {
   155→    text: text.trim(),
   156→    collectedEntities,
   157→    collectedRelationships,
   158→    toolCalls,
   159→  };
   160→}
   161→
   162→export const runGraphAwareChat = runChatAgent;
   163→
   164→type KnownEntity = { entityId: string; kind: string; name: string };
   165→
   166→/**
   167→ * Replace entity name mentions in response text with markdown links:
   168→ *   "Model for Extraction Pipeline" → "[Model for Extraction Pipeline](entity://decision:uuid)"
   169→ *
   170→ * Matches longest names first to avoid partial replacement.
   171→ * Only replaces the first occurrence of each entity name.
   172→ */
   173→function injectEntityLinks(text: string, context: ChatContext): string {
   174→  const entities: KnownEntity[] = [];
   175→
   176→  for (const project of context.workspaceSummary.projects) {
   177→    entities.push({ entityId: `project:${project.id}`, kind: "project", name: project.name });
   178→  }
   179→  for (const decision of context.workspaceSummary.recentDecisions) {
   180→    entities.push({ entityId: `decision:${decision.id}`, kind: "decision", name: decision.name });
   181→  }
   182→  for (const question of context.workspaceSummary.openQuestions) {
   183→    entities.push({ entityId: `question:${question.id}`, kind: "question", name: question.name });
   184→  }
   185→
   186→  // Sort longest name first so "Agent-Native Business OS" matches before "Agent"
   187→  entities.sort((a, b) => b.name.length - a.name.length);
   188→
   189→  const replaced = new Set<string>();
   190→  let result = text;
   191→
   192→  for (const entity of entities) {
   193→    if (entity.name.length < 3) continue;
   194→    if (replaced.has(entity.entityId)) continue;
   195→
   196→    // Case-insensitive match, but only replace the first occurrence.
   197→    // Skip if the name is already inside a markdown link.
   198→    const idx = result.toLowerCase().indexOf(entity.name.toLowerCase());
   199→    if (idx === -1) continue;
   200→
   201→    // Check if already inside a markdown link: [...](...)
   202→    const before = result.slice(0, idx);
   203→    if (before.lastIndexOf("[") > before.lastIndexOf("]")) continue;
   204→
   205→    const matched = result.slice(idx, idx + entity.name.length);
   206→    const link = `[${matched}](#entity/${entity.entityId})`;
   207→    result = `${result.slice(0, idx)}${link}${result.slice(idx + entity.name.length)}`;
   208→    replaced.add(entity.entityId);
   209→  }
   210→
   211→  return result;
   212→}
   213→
```

```
Perfect! Now I have the complete picture. Let me create a comprehensive summary of my findings:

## Summary: PM Agent Claims vs. Actual Persistence

I've thoroughly investigated the PM agent architecture and identified the **root cause** of the disconnect between claimed creation and actual database persistence.

### Key Findings

**1. PM Agent Architecture is "Suggestions-Only" by Design**

The PM agent uses Vercel AI SDK's `ToolLoopAgent` with a critical pattern:
- Line 83 in `agent.ts`: `output: Output.object({ schema: pmAgentResultSchema })`
- The agent is configured to return **structured output only**, not to execute tool calls

The `pmAgentResultSchema` has fields: `summary`, `suggestions`, `updated`, `discarded`, `observations_created` — these are **descriptions of what the agent thinks should happen**, not actual execution results.

**2. PM Agent Never Actually Calls `create_work_item`**

Looking at the PM agent prompt (`prompt.ts`), the instructions explicitly state:
```
"Direct creation (create_work_item): user explicitly says 'add/create/make a task for X'..."
"Suggestions (suggest_work_items): user is discussing/brainstorming, PM agent infers potential work items..."
```

The PM agent is designed to:
- Call `suggest_work_items` (returns triage buckets, does NOT persist)
- Rarely call `create_work_item` (only for explicit user requests)
- Return its output as structured JSON (not tool results)

**3. Tool Execution vs. Output Generation**

The ToolLoopAgent in this configuration generates tool calls during reasoning but formats them into the final `pmAgentResultSchema` output. The actual tool execution happens, but:
- Results are captured in `result.steps[].toolResults` (line 98 in `agent.ts`)
- Extracted entities from tool outputs are collected
- **But the tools aren't directly creating database records** — they return suggestions/analysis

**4. Critical Issue: `experimental_context` is Passed Correctly**

Line 86: `experimental_context: { ...input.context, actor: "pm_agent" as const }`

The context includes:
- `workspaceRecord`, `personRecord`, `currentMessageRecord`
- `surreal` connection via `deps`
- `actor: "pm_agent"` for authority checks

Tools CAN write to the database because they receive `deps.surreal`. The issue is **the PM agent's instructions discourage direct creation** — it's meant to propose/suggest, not directly persist.

**5. The Actual Persistence Gap**

When PM agent calls `suggest_work_items`:
- It returns `{ suggestions: [], updated: [], discarded: [] }`
- These are **unpersisted proposals**
- The chat agent renders them as UI suggestions for the user to accept
- Only when user accepts via `POST /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/work-items/accept` are they persisted

The PM agent says "I created X" but what actually happened:
- It called `suggest_work_items` which returned a **proposal**
- No database write occurred
- The UI shows it as a pending suggestion, not a created entity

### Code Evidence

**File:** `/Users/marcus/Git/brain/app/src/server/agents/pm/agent.ts`
- Lines 83-88: ToolLoopAgent configured with `Output.object()` schema
- Lines 95-98: Tool results captured but formatted into output schema, not returned directly

**File:** `/Users/marcus/Git/brain/app/src/server/agents/pm/prompt.ts`
- Lines 39-41: Clear distinction between direct creation (rare) vs suggestions (common)
- The system prompt actively guides away from `create_work_item` in brainstorming scenarios

**File:** `/Users/marcus/Git/brain/app/src/server/chat/tools/suggest-work-items.ts`
- This tool returns triage analysis only; it does NOT persist to database
- Dedup/analysis happens in-memory only

### Conclusion

The PM agent is **working as designed** — it proposes/suggests work items for human review. The "claim" to have created entities is an artifact of the output schema formatting, not actual persistence. To fix "missing entities," you need to either:

1. Accept PM suggestions via the work-item-accept endpoint
2. Modify PM agent instructions to use `create_work_item` more aggressively (requires authority permission checks)
3. Have chat agent accept PM suggestions automatically (not recommended without human approval)
```

```
     7→
     8→const workItemSuggestionSchema = z
     9→  .object({
    10→    kind: z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]),
    11→    title: z.string().min(1),
    12→    rationale: z.string().min(1),
    13→    category: z.enum(["none", ...ENTITY_CATEGORIES]),
    14→    project: z.enum(["none"]).or(z.string().min(1)),
    15→    priority: z.enum(["none", ...ENTITY_PRIORITIES]),
    16→    possible_duplicate: z.enum(["none"]).or(
    17→      z.object({
    18→        id: z.string().min(1),
    19→        name: z.string().min(1),
    20→        similarity: z.number().min(0).max(1),
    21→      }),
    22→    ),
    23→  })
    24→  .transform((v) => ({
    25→    ...v,
    26→    category: v.category === "none" ? undefined : v.category,
    27→    project: v.project === "none" ? undefined : v.project,
    28→    priority: v.priority === "none" ? undefined : v.priority,
    29→    possible_duplicate: v.possible_duplicate === "none" ? undefined : v.possible_duplicate,
    30→  }));
    31→
    32→const pmAgentResultSchema = z.object({
    33→  summary: z.string().min(1),
    34→  suggestions: z.array(workItemSuggestionSchema),
    35→  updated: z.array(
    36→    z.object({
    37→      existing_id: z.string().min(1),
    38→      title: z.string().min(1),
    39→      changes: z.string().min(1),
    40→    }),
    41→  ),
    42→  discarded: z.array(
    43→    z.object({
    44→      title: z.string().min(1),
    45→      reason: z.string().min(1),
    46→    }),
    47→  ),
    48→  observations_created: z.array(z.string().min(1)),
    49→});
    50→
    51→export type WorkItemSuggestion = z.infer<typeof workItemSuggestionSchema>;
    52→export type PmAgentResult = z.infer<typeof pmAgentResultSchema>;
    53→
    54→export type PmAgentOutput = PmAgentResult & {
    55→  extracted_entities: ExtractedEntity[];
    56→  extracted_relationships: ExtractedRelationship[];
    57→};
    58→
    59→export type PmAgentInput = {
    60→  deps: ChatToolDeps & { pmAgentModel: any };
    61→  context: ChatToolExecutionContext;
    62→  intent: "plan_work" | "check_status" | "organize" | "track_dependencies";
    63→  conversationContext: string;
    64→  project?: string;
    65→};
    66→
    67→const INTENT_INSTRUCTIONS: Record<PmAgentInput["intent"], string> = {
    68→  check_status: "Primary action: call get_project_status when a project scope is available.",
    69→  plan_work: "Primary action: propose tasks/features/projects with suggest_work_items or create_work_item, deduping each item.",
    70→  track_dependencies: "Primary action: identify blockers/dependencies and create observations for high-risk paths.",
    71→  organize: "Primary action: organize work into clear, deduplicated next steps.",
    72→};
    73→
    74→export async function runPmAgent(input: PmAgentInput): Promise<PmAgentOutput> {
    75→  const system = await buildPmSystemPrompt({
    76→    surreal: input.deps.surreal,
    77→    workspaceRecord: input.context.workspaceRecord,
    78→  });
    79→
    80→  const agent = new ToolLoopAgent({
    81→    id: "pm-agent",
    82→    model: input.deps.pmAgentModel,
    83→    instructions: system,
    84→    tools: createPmTools(input.deps),
    85→    output: Output.object({ schema: pmAgentResultSchema }),
    86→    experimental_context: { ...input.context, actor: "pm_agent" as const },
    87→    stopWhen: stepCountIs(6),
    88→  });
    89→
    90→  const result = await agent.generate({
    91→    prompt: [
    92→      "You are handling a PM request.",
    93→      `Intent: ${input.intent}`,
    94→      input.project ? `Project hint: ${input.project}` : "Project hint: not provided",
    95→      INTENT_INSTRUCTIONS[input.intent],
    96→      "Context:",
    97→      input.conversationContext,
    98→    ].join("\n"),
    99→  });
   100→
   101→  if (!result.output) {
   102→    throw new Error("pm agent did not produce structured output");
   103→  }
   104→
   105→  const extracted_entities: ExtractedEntity[] = [];
   106→  const extracted_relationships: ExtractedRelationship[] = [];
   107→
   108→  for (const step of result.steps) {
   109→    for (const toolResult of step.toolResults) {
   110→      const res = toolResult.output as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   111→      if (res) {
   112→        if (Array.isArray(res.extracted_entities)) {
   113→          extracted_entities.push(...(res.extracted_entities as ExtractedEntity[]));
   114→        }
   115→        if (Array.isArray(res.extracted_relationships)) {
   116→          extracted_relationships.push(...(res.extracted_relationships as ExtractedRelationship[]));
   117→        }
   118→      }
   119→    }
   120→  }
   121→
   122→  return {
   123→    ...result.output,
   124→    extracted_entities,
   125→    extracted_relationships,
   126→  };
   127→}
   128→
```

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";

     5→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";

     7→
     8→export function createInvokePmAgentTool(deps: ChatAgentToolDeps) {
     9→  return tool({
    10→    description:
    11→      "Invoke the PM subagent — the single authority on tasks, features, and project status.",
    12→    inputSchema: z.object({
    13→      intent: z
    14→        .enum(["plan_work", "check_status", "organize", "track_dependencies"])
    15→        .describe(
    16→          "plan_work: user discusses goals, features, or work to be done. check_status: user asks about progress, blockers, or project status. organize: user wants to restructure or re-prioritize. track_dependencies: user asks about blocked items or dependency chains.",
    17→        ),
    18→      context: z.string().min(1).describe("Conversation context for the PM agent"),
    19→      project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project scope"),
    20→    }),
    21→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    22→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    23→
    24→      try {
    25→        return await runPmAgent({
    26→          deps,
    27→          context,
    28→          intent: input.intent,
    29→          conversationContext: input.context,
    30→          ...(input.project ? { project: input.project } : {}),
    31→        });
    32→      } catch (error) {
    33→        logError("tool.invoke_pm_agent.failed", "PM agent invocation failed", error, {
    34→          intent: input.intent,
    35→          workspace: context.workspaceRecord.toString(),
    36→          conversation: context.conversationRecord.toString(),
    37→        });
    38→        throw error;
    39→      }
    40→    },
    41→  });
    42→}
```

```
     7→import {
     8→  createExtractionProvenanceEdge,

    10→  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,

    16→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    17→
    18→export function createCreateWorkItemTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    19→  return tool({
    20→    description:
    21→      "Create a task, feature, or project directly in the knowledge graph. Use when the user explicitly requests creation (\"add a task for X\") or during onboarding entity seeding. For uncertain/brainstormed items, prefer suggest_work_items instead.",
    22→    inputSchema: z.object({
    23→      kind: z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]).describe(
    24→        "task: concrete executable work with action verb. feature: capability/requirement within a project. project: named top-level workstream.",
    25→      ),
    26→      title: z.string().min(1).describe("Concise entity title"),
    27→      rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why this entity is needed — seeds the description"),
    28→      category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Category classification"),
    29→      priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional().describe("critical: blocking/urgent. high: important, needs attention soon. medium: normal priority. low: nice-to-have, deferred."),
    30→      project: z.string().optional().describe("Project name to scope the entity under"),
    31→    }),
    32→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    33→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_task", deps);
    34→      const now = new Date();
    35→
    36→      const embedding = await createEmbeddingVector(
    37→        deps.embeddingModel,
    38→        input.title,
    39→        deps.embeddingDimension,
    40→      );
    41→
    42→      const entityId = randomUUID();
    43→
    44→      if (input.kind === "project") {
    45→        const projectRecord = await createProjectRecord({
    46→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    47→          name: input.title,
    48→          status: "active",
    49→          now,
    50→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    51→          sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    52→        });
    53→
    54→        if (embedding) {
    55→          await deps.surreal.update(projectRecord).merge({ embedding });
    56→        }
    57→
    58→        void seedDescriptionEntry({
    59→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    60→          targetRecord: projectRecord,
    61→          text: input.rationale,
    62→        }).catch(() => undefined);
    63→
    64→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
    65→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    66→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    67→          targetRecord: projectRecord,
    68→          now,
    69→          confidence: 0.95,
    70→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
    71→          fromText: input.title,
    72→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
    73→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    74→        });
    75→
    76→        return {
    77→          entity_id: `project:${projectRecord.id as string}`,
    78→          kind: "project" as const,
    79→          title: input.title,
    80→        };
    81→      }
    82→
    83→      if (input.kind === "task") {
    84→        const taskRecord = new RecordId("task", entityId);
    85→        await deps.surreal.create(taskRecord).content({
    86→          title: input.title,
    87→          status: "open",
    88→          workspace: context.workspaceRecord,
    89→          ...(input.category ? { category: input.category } : {}),
    90→          ...(input.priority ? { priority: input.priority } : {}),
    91→          ...(embedding ? { embedding } : {}),
    92→          created_at: now,
    93→          updated_at: now,
    94→        });
    95→
    96→        if (input.project) {
    97→          try {
    98→            const projectRecord = await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    99→              surreal: deps.surreal,
   100→              workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   101→              projectInput: input.project,
   102→            });
   103→            await deps.surreal
   104→              .relate(taskRecord, new RecordId("belongs_to", randomUUID()), projectRecord, {
   105→                added_at: now,
   106→              })
   107→              .output("after");
   108→          } catch {
   109→            // project resolution is best-effort
   110→          }
   111→        }
   112→
   113→        void seedDescriptionEntry({
   114→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   115→          targetRecord: taskRecord,
   116→          text: input.rationale,
   117→        }).catch(() => undefined);
   118→
   119→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
   120→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   121→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   122→          targetRecord: taskRecord,
   123→          now,
   124→          confidence: 0.95,
   125→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
   126→          fromText: input.title,
   127→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
   128→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   129→        });
   130→
   131→        return {

   178→      await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
```

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES, ENTITY_PRIORITIES, type EntityCategory } from "../../../shared/contracts";
     4→import { createEmbeddingVector } from "../../graph/embeddings";
     5→import { resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord, searchEntitiesByEmbedding } from "../../graph/queries";
     6→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../../iam/authority";
     7→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     8→
     9→type SuggestedWorkItem = {
    10→  kind: "task" | "feature" | "project";
    11→  title: string;
    12→  rationale: string;
    13→  category?: EntityCategory;
    14→  project?: string;
    15→  priority?: string;
    16→  possible_duplicate?: {
    17→    id: string;
    18→    name: string;
    19→    similarity: number;
    20→  };
    21→};
    22→
    23→type UpdatedWorkItem = {
    24→  existing_id: string;
    25→  title: string;
    26→  changes: string;
    27→};
    28→
    29→type DiscardedWorkItem = {
    30→  title: string;
    31→  reason: string;
    32→};
    33→
    34→const CLOSED_STATUSES = new Set(["done", "completed", "closed", "resolved", "superseded"]);
    35→
    36→export function createSuggestWorkItemsTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    37→  return tool({
    38→    description:
    39→      "Process a batch of proposed work items and return issue-style PM triage buckets: suggestions, updated, discarded.",
    40→    inputSchema: z.object({
    41→      items: z.array(
    42→        z.object({
    43→          kind: z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]).describe("Work item kind"),
    44→          title: z.string().min(1).describe("Concise work item title"),
    45→          rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why this work item is needed"),
    46→          category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Optional work item category"),
    47→          project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project scope"),
    48→          priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional().describe("critical: blocking/urgent. high: important. medium: normal. low: nice-to-have."),
    49→        }),
    50→      ).min(1).max(25),
    51→    }),
    52→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    53→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_suggestion", deps);
    54→      const suggestions: SuggestedWorkItem[] = [];
    55→      const updated: UpdatedWorkItem[] = [];
    56→      const discarded: DiscardedWorkItem[] = [];
    57→
    58→      for (const item of input.items) {
    59→        const suggestion: SuggestedWorkItem = {
    60→          kind: item.kind,
    61→          title: item.title,
    62→          rationale: item.rationale,
    63→          ...(item.category ? { category: item.category } : {}),
    64→          ...(item.project ? { project: item.project } : {}),
    65→          ...(item.priority ? { priority: item.priority } : {}),
    66→        };
    67→
    68→        const embedding = await createEmbeddingVector(deps.embeddingModel, item.title, deps.embeddingDimension);
    69→        if (!embedding) {
    70→          throw new Error("failed to create embedding for suggest_work_items");
    71→        }
    72→
    73→        const projectRecord = item.project
    74→          ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    75→              surreal: deps.surreal,
    76→              workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    77→              projectInput: item.project,
    78→            })
    79→          : undefined;
    80→
    81→        const candidates = await searchEntitiesByEmbedding({
    82→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    83→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    84→          queryEmbedding: embedding,
    85→          kinds: [item.kind],
    86→          ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),
    87→          limit: 5,
    88→        });
    89→
    90→        const top = candidates[0];
    91→        if (!top) {
    92→          suggestions.push(suggestion);
    93→          continue;
    94→        }
    95→
    96→        const normalizedTopStatus = top.status ? top.status.trim().toLowerCase() : undefined;
    97→        if (normalizedTopStatus && CLOSED_STATUSES.has(normalizedTopStatus)) {
    98→          suggestions.push(suggestion);
    99→          continue;
   100→        }
   101→
   102→        if (top.score > 0.97) {
   103→          discarded.push({
   104→            title: item.title,
   105→            reason: `Exact duplicate of existing item ${top.kind}:${top.id} (${top.name})`,
   106→          });
   107→          continue;
   108→        }
   109→
   110→        if (top.score >= 0.8) {
   111→          updated.push({
   112→            existing_id: `${top.kind}:${top.id}`,
   113→            title: top.name,
   114→            changes: `Merge context from suggested item '${item.title}'`,
   115→          });
   116→          continue;
   117→        }
   118→
   119→        suggestions.push(suggestion);
   120→      }
   121→
   122→      return {
   123→        suggestions,
   124→        updated,
   125→        discarded,
   126→      };
   127→    },
   128→  });
   129→}
   130→
```

> AGENT

Now I have the full picture. Here's the root cause:

**The chat agent doesn't have `create_work_item` as a direct tool** — it can only create work items via `invoke_pm_agent`. The PM agent (GPT-4.1-mini) then either skips tool calls entirely and generates its structured output directly, or calls `suggest_work_items` (which doesn't persist). Either way, nothing gets written to the database, but the chat agent relays the PM's summary as if creation happened.

The fix: give the chat agent `create_work_item` directly and update the onboarding instructions to use it instead of dispatching through the PM agent.

> AGENT

The changes look right. Summary of the three fixes:

1. **`agent.ts` intent instruction**: `plan_work` now says "create with `create_work_item`" first (was "propose with `suggest_work_items` or `create_work_item`"). Also adds "You MUST call create_work_item before generating output."

2. **`agent.ts` generate prompt**: Added explicit instruction that tools MUST be called before generating final output — prevents GPT-4.1-mini from skipping tools and going straight to structured output.

3. **`prompt.ts` system prompt**: 
   - "Direct creation" is now the DEFAULT for clearly described items (was only for explicit "add/create" commands)
   - "Suggestions" is now ONLY for vague/uncertain items (was the default path for brainstorming)
   - Added workspace name guard to prevent creating a project with the same name as the workspace

The root cause was: GPT-4.1-mini as the PM agent was either skipping tools entirely (going straight to structured output claiming creation) or defaulting to `suggest_work_items` (which triages but doesn't persist). The prompt favored `suggest_work_items` by listing it first and making direct creation seem like an exception case.
