    14→
    15→export function createChatIngressHandlers(deps: ServerDependencies): {
    16→  handlePostChatMessage: (request: Request) => Promise<Response>;
    17→  handleChatStream: (messageId: string) => Response;
    18→} {
    19→  return {
    20→    handlePostChatMessage: (request: Request) => handlePostChatMessage(deps, request),
    21→    handleChatStream: (messageId: string) => deps.sse.handleStreamRequest(messageId),
    22→  };
    23→}
    24→
    25→async function handlePostChatMessage(deps: ServerDependencies, request: Request): Promise<Response> {
    26→  const startedAt = performance.now();
    27→  logInfo("chat.message.ingress.started", "Chat message ingress started");
    28→
    29→  let parsed: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof parseIncomingMessageRequest>>;
    30→  try {
    31→    parsed = await parseIncomingMessageRequest(request);
    32→  } catch (error) {
    33→    logError("chat.message.parse.failed", "Parsing incoming chat message failed", error);
    34→    const errorText = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "invalid request body";
    35→    return jsonError(errorText, 400);
    36→  }
    37→
    38→  if (!parsed.ok) {
    39→    return jsonError(parsed.error, 400);
    40→  }
    41→
    42→  const conversationId = parsed.data.conversationId ?? randomUUID();
    43→  const messageId = randomUUID();
    44→  const workspaceId = parsed.data.workspaceId;
    45→  const userText = parsed.data.text.trim();
    46→  const onboardingAction = parsed.data.onboardingAction;
    47→  const messageText = userText.length > 0 ? userText : `Uploaded document: ${parsed.data.attachment?.fileName ?? "attachment"}`;
    48→  const userMessageRecord = new RecordId("message", randomUUID());
    49→
    50→  logDebug("http.request.validated", "Chat message request validated", {
    51→    workspaceId,
    52→    conversationId,
    53→    hasAttachment: parsed.data.attachment !== undefined,
    54→  });
    55→
    56→  let workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    57→
    58→  try {
    59→    logInfo("chat.message.persist.started", "Persisting user chat message", {
    60→      workspaceId,
    61→      conversationId,
    62→      messageId,
    63→    });
    64→
    65→    workspaceRecord = await resolveWorkspaceRecord(deps.surreal, workspaceId);
    66→    const workspace = await deps.surreal.select<WorkspaceRow>(workspaceRecord);
    67→    if (!workspace) {
    68→      throw new HttpError(404, `workspace not found: ${workspaceId}`);
    69→    }
    70→
    71→    const discussEntityTables = ["project", "person", "feature", "task", "decision", "question", "observation"] as const;
    72→    const discussesRecord = parsed.data.discussEntityId
    73→      ? parseRecordIdString(parsed.data.discussEntityId, [...discussEntityTables])
    74→      : undefined;
    75→
    76→    const now = new Date();
    77→    const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", conversationId);
    78→    const existingConversation = await deps.surreal.select<ConversationRow>(conversationRecord);
    79→
    80→    const transaction = await deps.surreal.beginTransaction();
    81→    try {
    82→      if (existingConversation) {
    83→        if (!existingConversation.workspace) {
    84→          throw new HttpError(500, "conversation is missing workspace scope");
    85→        }
    86→
    87→        if (existingConversation.workspace.id !== workspaceRecord.id) {
    88→          throw new HttpError(400, "conversation scope does not match workspaceId");
    89→        }
    90→
    91→        await transaction.update(conversationRecord).merge({
    92→          updatedAt: now,
    93→        });
    94→      } else {
    95→        await transaction.create(conversationRecord).content({
    96→          createdAt: now,
    97→          updatedAt: now,
    98→          workspace: workspaceRecord,
    99→          title: deriveMessageTitle(messageText),
   100→          title_source: "message",
   101→          ...(workspace.onboarding_complete ? {} : { source: "onboarding" }),
   102→          ...(discussesRecord ? { discusses: discussesRecord } : {}),
   103→        });
   104→      }
   105→
   106→      await transaction.create(userMessageRecord).content({
   107→        conversation: conversationRecord,
   108→        role: "user",
   109→        text: messageText,
   110→        createdAt: now,
   111→        clientMessageId: parsed.data.clientMessageId,
   112→      });
   113→
   114→      if (!workspace.onboarding_complete) {
   115→        await transaction.update(workspaceRecord).merge({
   116→          onboarding_turn_count: workspace.onboarding_turn_count + 1,
   117→          updated_at: now,
   118→        });
   119→      }
   120→
   121→      await transaction.commit();
   122→    } catch (error) {
   123→      await transaction.cancel();
   124→      throw error;
   125→    }
   126→
   127→    logInfo("chat.message.persist.completed", "User chat message persisted", {
   128→      workspaceId,
   129→      conversationId,
   130→      messageId,
   131→      userMessageId: userMessageRecord.id as string,
   132→    });
   133→  } catch (error) {
   134→    if (error instanceof HttpError) {
   135→      logWarn("chat.message.persist.http_error", "Chat message persistence failed with client-facing error", {
   136→        workspaceId,
   137→        conversationId,
   138→        messageId,
   139→        statusCode: error.status,
   140→      });
   141→      return jsonError(error.message, error.status);
   142→    }
   143→
   144→    logError("chat.message.persist.failed", "Persisting user chat message failed", error, {
   145→      workspaceId,
   146→      conversationId,
   147→      messageId,
   148→    });
   149→    const errorText = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "failed to persist user message";
   150→    return jsonError(errorText, 500);
   151→  }
   152→
   153→  deps.sse.registerMessage(messageId);
   154→
   155→  const session = await deps.auth.api.getSession({ headers: request.headers });
   156→  if (!session?.user?.id) {
   157→    return jsonError("authentication required", 401);
   158→  }
   159→  const personRecord = new RecordId("person", session.user.id);
   160→
   161→  logInfo("chat.message.process.started", "Async chat processing started", {
   162→    workspaceId,
   163→    conversationId,
   164→    messageId,
   165→  });
   166→
   167→  void processChatMessage({
   168→    deps,
   169→    conversationId,
   170→    messageId,
   171→    workspaceRecord,
   172→    userMessageRecord,
   173→    userText: messageText,
   174→    attachment: parsed.data.attachment,
   175→    ...(onboardingAction ? { onboardingAction } : {}),
   176→    personRecord,
   177→  });
   178→
   179→  const response: ChatMessageResponse = {
   180→    messageId,
   181→    userMessageId: userMessageRecord.id as string,
   182→    conversationId,
   183→    workspaceId,
   184→    streamUrl: `/api/chat/stream/${messageId}`,
   185→  };
   186→
   187→  logInfo("chat.message.ingress.completed", "Chat message ingress completed", {
   188→    workspaceId,
   189→    conversationId,
   190→    messageId,
   191→    durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
   192→  });
   193→
   194→  return jsonResponse(response, 200);
   195→}
   196→

     1→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     2→
     3→export type BranchLink = {
     4→  conversationId: string;
     5→  parentConversationId: string;
     6→  branchPointMessageId: string;
     7→  branchPointCreatedAt: Date;
     8→};
     9→
    10→export type InheritableMessage = {
    11→  id: string;
    12→  role: "user" | "assistant";
    13→  text: string;
    14→  createdAt: Date | string;
    15→  suggestions?: string[];
    16→  inherited: boolean;
    17→};
    18→
    19→type MessageRow = {
    20→  id: RecordId<"message", string>;
    21→  role: "user" | "assistant";
    22→  text: string;
    23→  createdAt: Date | string;
    24→  suggestions?: string[];
    25→};
    26→
    27→/**
    28→ * Walk branched_from edges from a conversation up to the root.
    29→ * Returns an ordered array (root-first) of branch links.
    30→ */
    31→export async function loadBranchChain(
    32→  surreal: Surreal,
    33→  conversationId: string,
    34→): Promise<BranchLink[]> {
    35→  const chain: BranchLink[] = [];
    36→  let current = conversationId;
    37→
    38→  for (let depth = 0; depth < 10; depth++) {
    39→    const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", current);
    40→    const [rows] = await surreal
    41→      .query<[Array<{
    42→        out: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
    43→        branch_point_message: RecordId<"message", string>;
    44→      }>]>(
    45→        "SELECT out, branch_point_message FROM branched_from WHERE `in` = $conversation LIMIT 1;",
    46→        { conversation: conversationRecord },
    47→      )
    48→      .collect<[Array<{
    49→        out: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
    50→        branch_point_message: RecordId<"message", string>;
    51→      }>]>();
    52→
    53→    if (rows.length === 0) break;
    54→
    55→    const edge = rows[0];
    56→    const branchPointMsg = await surreal.select<{ createdAt: Date | string }>(edge.branch_point_message);
    57→    if (!branchPointMsg) {
    58→      throw new Error(`branch point message not found: ${edge.branch_point_message.id as string}`);
    59→    }
    60→
    61→    chain.unshift({
    62→      conversationId: current,
    63→      parentConversationId: edge.out.id as string,
    64→      branchPointMessageId: edge.branch_point_message.id as string,
    65→      branchPointCreatedAt: branchPointMsg.createdAt instanceof Date
    66→        ? branchPointMsg.createdAt
    67→        : new Date(branchPointMsg.createdAt),
    68→    });
    69→
    70→    current = edge.out.id as string;
    71→  }
    72→
    73→  return chain;
    74→}
    75→
    76→/**
    77→ * Load all messages for a conversation including inherited messages from ancestor branches.
    78→ * Inherited messages are marked with `inherited: true`.
    79→ */
    80→export async function loadMessagesWithInheritance(
    81→  surreal: Surreal,
    82→  conversationId: string,
    83→  limit: number,
    84→): Promise<InheritableMessage[]> {
    85→  const chain = await loadBranchChain(surreal, conversationId);
    86→
    87→  if (chain.length === 0) {
    88→    const messages = await queryConversationMessages(surreal, conversationId, limit);
    89→    return messages.map((m) => toInheritable(m, false));
    90→  }
    91→
    92→  const allMessages: InheritableMessage[] = [];
    93→
    94→  // Root conversation messages up to first branch point
    95→  const rootConvId = chain[0].parentConversationId;
    96→  const rootMessages = await queryMessagesUpToBranchPoint(
    97→    surreal,
    98→    rootConvId,
    99→    chain[0].branchPointCreatedAt,
   100→  );
   101→  allMessages.push(...rootMessages.map((m) => toInheritable(m, true)));
   102→
   103→  // Intermediate ancestor messages between branch points
   104→  for (let i = 0; i < chain.length - 1; i++) {
   105→    const intermediateMessages = await queryMessagesUpToBranchPoint(
   106→      surreal,
   107→      chain[i].conversationId,
   108→      chain[i + 1].branchPointCreatedAt,
   109→    );
   110→    allMessages.push(...intermediateMessages.map((m) => toInheritable(m, true)));
   111→  }
   112→
   113→  // Current conversation's own messages
   114→  const ownMessages = await queryConversationMessages(surreal, conversationId, limit);
   115→  allMessages.push(...ownMessages.map((m) => toInheritable(m, false)));
   116→
   117→  return allMessages;
   118→}
   119→
   120→function toInheritable(row: MessageRow, inherited: boolean): InheritableMessage {
   121→  return {
   122→    id: row.id.id as string,
   123→    role: row.role,
   124→    text: row.text,
   125→    createdAt: row.createdAt,
   126→    ...(row.suggestions && row.suggestions.length > 0 ? { suggestions: row.suggestions } : {}),
   127→    inherited,
   128→  };
   129→}
   130→
   131→async function queryConversationMessages(
   132→  surreal: Surreal,
   133→  conversationId: string,
   134→  limit: number,
   135→): Promise<MessageRow[]> {
   136→  const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", conversationId);
   137→  const [rows] = await surreal
   138→    .query<[MessageRow[]]>(
   139→      "SELECT id, role, text, createdAt, suggestions FROM message WHERE conversation = $conversation ORDER BY createdAt ASC LIMIT $limit;",
   140→      { conversation: conversationRecord, limit },
   141→    )
   142→    .collect<[MessageRow[]]>();
   143→  return rows;
   144→}
   145→
   146→/**
   147→ * Load messages from a conversation up to and including the branch point message
   148→ * and its assistant response (the next message after the branch point).
   149→ */
   150→async function queryMessagesUpToBranchPoint(
   151→  surreal: Surreal,
   152→  conversationId: string,
   153→  branchPointCreatedAt: Date,
   154→): Promise<MessageRow[]> {
   155→  const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", conversationId);
   156→
   157→  // Find the assistant response following the branch point (if any)
   158→  const [nextRows] = await surreal
   159→    .query<[MessageRow[]]>(
   160→      "SELECT id, role, text, createdAt, suggestions FROM message WHERE conversation = $conversation AND createdAt > $branchPointAt ORDER BY createdAt ASC LIMIT 1;",
   161→      { conversation: conversationRecord, branchPointAt: branchPointCreatedAt },
   162→    )
   163→    .collect<[MessageRow[]]>();
   164→
   165→  const cutoffAt = nextRows.length > 0 && nextRows[0].role === "assistant"
   166→    ? (nextRows[0].createdAt instanceof Date ? nextRows[0].createdAt : new Date(nextRows[0].createdAt as string))
   167→    : branchPointCreatedAt;
   168→
   169→  const [rows] = await surreal
   170→    .query<[MessageRow[]]>(
   171→      "SELECT id, role, text, createdAt, suggestions FROM message WHERE conversation = $conversation AND createdAt <= $cutoff ORDER BY createdAt ASC;",
   172→      { conversation: conversationRecord, cutoff: cutoffAt },
   173→    )
   174→    .collect<[MessageRow[]]>();
   175→
   176→  return rows;
   177→}
   178→

   182→async function handleWorkspaceBootstrap(deps: ServerDependencies, workspaceId: string): Promise<Response> {
   183→  const startedAt = performance.now();
   184→  logInfo("workspace.bootstrap.started", "Workspace bootstrap started", { workspaceId });
   185→
   186→  try {
   187→    const workspaceRecord = await resolveWorkspaceRecord(deps.surreal, workspaceId);
   188→    const workspace = await deps.surreal.select<WorkspaceRow>(workspaceRecord);
   189→    if (!workspace) {
   190→      throw new HttpError(404, `workspace not found: ${workspaceId}`);
   191→    }
   192→
   193→    const conversationRecord = await resolveWorkspaceBootstrapConversation(deps, workspaceRecord);
   194→    const conversationId = conversationRecord.id as string;
   195→    const rawMessages = await loadMessagesWithInheritance(deps.surreal, conversationId, 80);
   196→
   197→    const messages = rawMessages.map((row) => ({
   198→      id: row.id,
   199→      role: row.role,
   200→      text: row.text,
   201→      createdAt: toIsoString(row.createdAt),
   202→      ...(row.suggestions && row.suggestions.length > 0 ? { suggestions: row.suggestions } : {}),
   203→      ...(row.inherited ? { inherited: true } : {}),
   204→    } satisfies WorkspaceBootstrapMessage));
   205→
   206→    const seeds = await loadWorkspaceSeeds(deps, workspaceRecord, 40);
   207→    const onboardingState = toOnboardingState(workspace);
   208→    const sidebar = await buildWorkspaceConversationSidebar(deps.surreal, workspaceRecord);
   209→
   210→    const payload: WorkspaceBootstrapResponse = {
   211→      workspaceId: workspace.id.id as string,
   212→      workspaceName: workspace.name,
   213→      ...(workspace.description ? { workspaceDescription: workspace.description } : {}),
   214→      onboardingComplete: workspace.onboarding_complete,
   215→      onboardingState,
   216→      conversationId: conversationRecord.id as string,
   217→      messages,
   218→      seeds,
   219→      sidebar,
   220→    };
   221→
   222→    logInfo("workspace.bootstrap.completed", "Workspace bootstrap completed", {
   223→      workspaceId,
   224→      conversationId: conversationRecord.id as string,
   225→      messageCount: messages.length,
   226→      seedCount: seeds.length,
   227→      durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
   228→    });
   229→
   230→    return jsonResponse(payload, 200);
   231→  } catch (error) {
   232→    if (error instanceof HttpError) {
   233→      logWarn("workspace.bootstrap.http_error", "Workspace bootstrap failed with client-facing error", {
   234→        workspaceId,
   235→        statusCode: error.status,
   236→      });
   237→      return jsonError(error.message, error.status);
   238→    }
   239→
   240→    logError("workspace.bootstrap.failed", "Workspace bootstrap failed", error, { workspaceId });
   241→    const errorText = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "workspace bootstrap failed";
   242→    return jsonError(errorText, 500);
   243→  }
   244→}
   245→
   246→async function resolveWorkspaceBootstrapConversation(
   247→  deps: ServerDependencies,
   248→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   249→): Promise<RecordId<"conversation", string>> {
   250→  const [onboardingRows] = await deps.surreal
   251→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"conversation", string> }>]>(
   252→      "SELECT id, createdAt FROM conversation WHERE workspace = $workspace AND source = 'onboarding' ORDER BY createdAt ASC LIMIT 1;",
   253→      {
   254→        workspace: workspaceRecord,
   255→      },
   256→    )
   257→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"conversation", string> }>]>();
   258→
   259→  if (onboardingRows.length > 0) {
   260→    return onboardingRows[0].id;
   261→  }
   262→
   263→  const [latestRows] = await deps.surreal
   264→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"conversation", string> }>]>(
   265→      "SELECT id, createdAt FROM conversation WHERE workspace = $workspace ORDER BY createdAt DESC LIMIT 1;",
   266→      {
   267→        workspace: workspaceRecord,
   268→      },
   269→    )
   270→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"conversation", string> }>]>();
   271→
   272→  if (latestRows.length > 0) {
   273→    return latestRows[0].id;
   274→  }
   275→
   276→  const now = new Date();
   277→  const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", randomUUID());
   278→  await deps.surreal.create(conversationRecord).content({
   279→    createdAt: now,
   280→    updatedAt: now,
   281→    workspace: workspaceRecord,

     1→import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
     2→import { useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
     3→import { useChat, type UseChatHelpers } from "@ai-sdk/react";
     4→import { DefaultChatTransport, type UIMessage } from "ai";
     5→import type {
     6→  BranchConversationResponse,
     7→  DiscussEntitySummary,
     8→  OnboardingAction,
     9→  OnboardingState,
    10→  WorkspaceBootstrapMessage,

    12→} from "../../shared/contracts";
    13→import { useWorkspaceState, type BootstrapPayload } from "../stores/workspace-state";

    15→
    16→type ChatMessageMetadata = {
    17→  onboardingState?: OnboardingState;
    18→  conversationId?: string;
    19→};
    20→
    21→type ChatUIMessage = UIMessage<ChatMessageMetadata>;
    22→
    23→export type UseChatSessionReturn = {
    24→  messages: ChatUIMessage[];
    25→  status: UseChatHelpers<ChatUIMessage>["status"];
    26→  isLoading: boolean;
    27→  errorMessage?: string;
    28→  activeConversationId?: string;
    29→  branchingFromId?: string;
    30→  discussEntity?: DiscussEntitySummary;
    31→  conversationDiscussEntity?: DiscussEntitySummary;
    32→  sendMessage: (text: string, options?: { onboardingAction?: OnboardingAction }) => void;
    33→  stopMessage: () => void;
    34→  newConversation: () => void;
    35→  resetChat: () => void;
    36→  selectConversation: (conversationId: string) => void;
    37→  branchFromMessage: (messageId: string) => Promise<void>;
    38→  setErrorMessage: (message: string | undefined) => void;
    39→  refreshSidebar: (workspaceId: string) => Promise<void>;
    40→};
    41→
    42→function bootstrapMessagesToUIMessages(messages: WorkspaceBootstrapMessage[]): ChatUIMessage[] {
    43→  return messages.map((m) => ({
    44→    id: m.id,
    45→    role: m.role,
    46→    parts: [{ type: "text" as const, text: m.text }],
    47→    createdAt: new Date(m.createdAt),
    48→  }));
    49→}
    50→
    51→export function useChatSession(routeConversationId?: string): UseChatSessionReturn {
    52→  const store = useWorkspaceState();
    53→  const workspaceId = store.workspaceId;
    54→  const bootstrapPayload = store.bootstrapPayload;
    55→  const navigate = useNavigate();
    56→
    57→  const [activeConversationId, setActiveConversationId] = useState<string | undefined>();
    58→  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | undefined>();
    59→  const [branchingFromId, setBranchingFromId] = useState<string | undefined>();
    60→  const [conversationDiscussEntity, setConversationDiscussEntity] = useState<
    61→    DiscussEntitySummary | undefined
    62→  >();
    63→
    64→  const discussEntity = useViewState((s) => s.discussEntity);
    65→  const clearDiscussEntity = useViewState((s) => s.clearDiscussEntity);
    66→
    67→  // Dynamic body refs — these are read by the transport body function
    68→  const conversationIdRef = useRef<string | undefined>(undefined);
    69→  const onboardingActionRef = useRef<OnboardingAction | undefined>(undefined);
    70→  const discussEntityIdRef = useRef<string | undefined>(undefined);
    71→
    72→  // Keep conversationId ref in sync
    73→  useEffect(() => {
    74→    conversationIdRef.current = activeConversationId;
    75→  }, [activeConversationId]);
    76→
    77→  // Keep discuss entity ref in sync
    78→  useEffect(() => {
    79→    discussEntityIdRef.current = discussEntity?.id;
    80→  }, [discussEntity]);
    81→
    82→  const transport = useMemo(
    83→    () =>
    84→      new DefaultChatTransport({
    85→        api: "/api/chat",
    86→        body: () => ({
    87→          workspaceId,
    88→          ...(conversationIdRef.current ? { conversationId: conversationIdRef.current } : {}),
    89→          ...(onboardingActionRef.current
    90→            ? { onboardingAction: onboardingActionRef.current }
    91→            : {}),
    92→          ...(discussEntityIdRef.current
    93→            ? { discussEntityId: discussEntityIdRef.current }
    94→            : {}),
    95→        }),
    96→      }),
    97→    [workspaceId],
    98→  );
    99→
   100→  const chat = useChat<ChatUIMessage>({
   101→    transport,
   102→    onFinish: ({ message }) => {
   103→      const metadata = message.metadata;
   104→      if (metadata?.conversationId && !conversationIdRef.current) {
   105→        setActiveConversationId(metadata.conversationId);
   106→        conversationIdRef.current = metadata.conversationId;
   107→        void navigate({
   108→          to: "/chat/$conversationId",
   109→          params: { conversationId: metadata.conversationId },
   110→          replace: true,
   111→        });
   112→        if (discussEntity) {
   113→          clearDiscussEntity();
   114→        }
   115→      }
   116→      if (metadata?.onboardingState) {
   117→        store.setOnboardingState(metadata.onboardingState);
   118→      }
   119→
   120→      // Refresh sidebar
   121→      if (workspaceId) {
   122→        void refreshSidebar(workspaceId);
   123→      }
   124→    },
   125→    onError: (error) => {
   126→      setErrorMessage(error.message);
   127→    },
   128→  });
   129→
   130→  const isLoading = chat.status === "streaming" || chat.status === "submitted";
   131→
   132→  // Apply bootstrap payload when it arrives — only load messages when on /chat/:id
   133→  const appliedBootstrapRef = useRef<BootstrapPayload | undefined>(undefined);
   134→  useEffect(() => {
   135→    if (!bootstrapPayload || bootstrapPayload === appliedBootstrapRef.current) {
   136→      return;
   137→    }
   138→    appliedBootstrapRef.current = bootstrapPayload;
   139→
   140→    // On /chat (new conversation), skip loading bootstrap messages
   141→    if (!routeConversationId) {
   142→      return;
   143→    }
   144→
   145→    chat.setMessages(bootstrapPayload.messages as ChatUIMessage[]);
   146→    setActiveConversationId(store.conversationId);
   147→    conversationIdRef.current = store.conversationId;
   148→  }, [bootstrapPayload, routeConversationId]);
   149→
   150→  async function refreshSidebar(wsId: string) {
   151→    try {
   152→      const response = await fetch(`/api/workspaces/${encodeURIComponent(wsId)}/sidebar`);
   153→      if (response.ok) {
   154→        const payload = await response.json();
   155→        store.setSidebar(payload);
   156→      }
   157→    } catch {
   158→      // Sidebar refresh is non-critical
   159→    }
   160→  }
   161→
   162→  async function loadConversation(wsId: string, conversationId: string) {
   163→    try {
   164→      const response = await fetch(
   165→        `/api/workspaces/${encodeURIComponent(wsId)}/conversations/${encodeURIComponent(conversationId)}`,
   166→      );
   167→      if (!response.ok) {
   168→        const body = await response.text();
   169→        setErrorMessage(body);
   170→        void navigate({ to: "/chat", replace: true });
   171→        return;
   172→      }
   173→
   174→      const payload = (await response.json()) as WorkspaceConversationResponse;
   175→      const uiMessages = bootstrapMessagesToUIMessages(payload.messages) as ChatUIMessage[];
   176→      chat.setMessages(uiMessages);
   177→
   178→      setActiveConversationId(conversationId);
   179→      conversationIdRef.current = conversationId;
   180→      setConversationDiscussEntity(payload.discussEntity);
   181→    } catch (error) {
   182→      const messageText = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Failed to load conversation";
   183→      setErrorMessage(messageText);
   184→    }
   185→  }
   186→
   187→  const sendMessage = useCallback(
   188→    (text: string, options?: { onboardingAction?: OnboardingAction }) => {
   189→      if (isLoading || !workspaceId) return;
   190→
   191→      const trimmed = text.trim();
   192→      if (!trimmed) return;
   193→
   194→      setErrorMessage(undefined);
   195→      onboardingActionRef.current = options?.onboardingAction;
   196→
   197→      chat.sendMessage({ text: trimmed });
   198→
   199→      // Clear after queueing
   200→      onboardingActionRef.current = undefined;
   201→    },
   202→    [isLoading, workspaceId, chat.sendMessage],
   203→  );
   204→
   205→  function stopMessage() {
   206→    chat.stop();
   207→  }
   208→
   209→  function newConversation() {
   210→    setActiveConversationId(undefined);
   211→    conversationIdRef.current = undefined;
   212→    chat.setMessages([]);
   213→    setErrorMessage(undefined);
   214→    setConversationDiscussEntity(undefined);
   215→    clearDiscussEntity();
   216→    void navigate({ to: "/chat" });
   217→  }
   218→
   219→  function resetChat() {
   220→    setActiveConversationId(undefined);
   221→    conversationIdRef.current = undefined;
   222→    chat.setMessages([]);
   223→    setErrorMessage(undefined);
   224→    setConversationDiscussEntity(undefined);
   225→  }
   226→
   227→  function selectConversation(conversationId: string) {
   228→    if (conversationId === activeConversationId || isLoading || !workspaceId) {
   229→      return;
   230→    }
   231→    clearDiscussEntity();
   232→    void loadConversation(workspaceId, conversationId);
   233→  }
   234→
   235→  async function branchFromMessage(messageId: string) {
   236→    if (!workspaceId || !activeConversationId || isLoading) return;
   237→
   238→    setBranchingFromId(messageId);
   239→    try {
   240→      const response = await fetch(
   241→        `/api/workspaces/${encodeURIComponent(workspaceId)}/conversations/${encodeURIComponent(activeConversationId)}/branch`,
   242→        {
   243→          method: "POST",
   244→          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   245→          body: JSON.stringify({ messageId }),
   246→        },
   247→      );
   248→
   249→      if (!response.ok) {
   250→        const body = await response.text();
   251→        setErrorMessage(body);
   252→        return;
   253→      }
   254→
   255→      const payload = (await response.json()) as BranchConversationResponse;
   256→      await loadConversation(workspaceId, payload.conversationId);
   257→      void navigate({
   258→        to: "/chat/$conversationId",
   259→        params: { conversationId: payload.conversationId },
   260→      });
   261→      await refreshSidebar(workspaceId);
   262→    } catch (error) {
   263→      const messageText = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Branch creation failed";
   264→      setErrorMessage(messageText);
   265→    } finally {
   266→      setBranchingFromId(undefined);
   267→    }
   268→  }
   269→
   270→  return {
   271→    messages: chat.messages,
   272→    status: chat.status,
   273→    isLoading,
   274→    errorMessage,
   275→    activeConversationId,
   276→    branchingFromId,
   277→    discussEntity,
   278→    conversationDiscussEntity,
   279→    sendMessage,
   280→    stopMessage,
   281→    newConversation,
   282→    resetChat,
   283→    selectConversation,
   284→    branchFromMessage,
   285→    setErrorMessage,
   286→    refreshSidebar,
   287→  };
   288→}
   289→
```

    18→};

    20→type WorkspaceStateStore = {

    23→  onboardingComplete: boolean;
    24→  onboardingState?: OnboardingState;
    25→  conversationId?: string;

    35→  setConversationId: (id: string | undefined) => void;
    36→  setSidebar: (sidebar: WorkspaceConversationSidebarResponse | undefined) => void;
    37→  setSeedItems: (items: OnboardingSeedItem[]) => void;
    38→  mergeSeedItems: (items: OnboardingSeedItem[]) => void;
    39→  setIsBootstrapping: (value: boolean) => void;
    40→  setSidebarHandlers: (handlers: SidebarHandlers | undefined) => void;

    45→    name: string;
    46→    onboardingComplete: boolean;
    47→    onboardingState: OnboardingState;
    48→    conversationId: string;
    49→  }) => void;
    50→};
    51→
    52→export const useWorkspaceState = create<WorkspaceStateStore>((set, get) => ({
    53→  workspaceId: undefined,
    54→  workspaceName: undefined,
    55→  onboardingComplete: false,
    56→  onboardingState: undefined,
    57→  conversationId: undefined,
    58→  sidebar: undefined,
    59→  seedItems: [],
    60→  isBootstrapping: false,
    61→  sidebarHandlers: undefined,
    62→  bootstrapPayload: undefined,

    64→  setWorkspaceId: (workspaceId) => set({ workspaceId }),

    69→  setSidebar: (sidebar) => set({ sidebar }),
    70→  setSeedItems: (seedItems) => set({ seedItems }),
    71→  mergeSeedItems: (items) => {
    72→    const existing = get().seedItems;
    73→    const deduped = new Map(
    74→      existing.map((item) => [`${item.id}:${item.sourceKind}:${item.sourceId}`, item]),
    75→    );
    76→    for (const item of items) {
    77→      const key = `${item.id}:${item.sourceKind}:${item.sourceId}`;
    78→      if (!deduped.has(key)) {
    79→        deduped.set(key, item);
    80→      }
    81→    }
    82→    set({ seedItems: [...deduped.values()] });
    83→  },
    84→  setIsBootstrapping: (isBootstrapping) => set({ isBootstrapping }),
    85→  setSidebarHandlers: (sidebarHandlers) => set({ sidebarHandlers }),
    86→  setBootstrapPayload: (bootstrapPayload) => set({ bootstrapPayload }),
    87→  clearWorkspace: () =>
    88→    set({

    91→      onboardingComplete: false,
    92→      onboardingState: undefined,
    93→      conversationId: undefined,
    94→      sidebar: undefined,

    97→      sidebarHandlers: undefined,
    98→      bootstrapPayload: undefined,

   104→      onboardingComplete: ws.onboardingComplete,
   105→      onboardingState: ws.onboardingState,
   106→      conversationId: ws.conversationId,

     1→import { FormEvent, useEffect, useState } from "react";
     2→import type { UIMessage } from "ai";
     3→import type {
     4→  CreateWorkspaceRequest,
     5→  CreateWorkspaceResponse,
     6→  WorkspaceBootstrapMessage,
     7→  WorkspaceBootstrapResponse,
     8→} from "../../shared/contracts";
     9→import { useWorkspaceState, type BootstrapPayload } from "../stores/workspace-state";
    10→
    11→const ACTIVE_WORKSPACE_STORAGE_KEY = "brain.activeWorkspaceId";
    12→
    13→type UseWorkspaceReturn = {
    14→  isReady: boolean;
    15→  isBootstrapping: boolean;
    16→  isCreatingWorkspace: boolean;
    17→  errorMessage?: string;
    18→  createWorkspaceName: string;
    19→  createWorkspaceDescription: string;
    20→  canCreateWorkspace: boolean;
    21→  setCreateWorkspaceName: (name: string) => void;
    22→  setCreateWorkspaceDescription: (description: string) => void;
    23→  onCreateWorkspace: (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => void;
    24→};
    25→
    26→function bootstrapMessagesToUIMessages(messages: WorkspaceBootstrapMessage[]): UIMessage[] {
    27→  return messages.map((m) => ({
    28→    id: m.id,
    29→    role: m.role,
    30→    parts: [{ type: "text" as const, text: m.text }],
    31→    createdAt: new Date(m.createdAt),
    32→  }));
    33→}
    34→
    35→function parseBootstrapMessages(payload: WorkspaceBootstrapResponse): BootstrapPayload {
    36→  return { messages: bootstrapMessagesToUIMessages(payload.messages) };
    37→}
    38→
    39→export { parseBootstrapMessages, ACTIVE_WORKSPACE_STORAGE_KEY };
    40→
    41→export function useWorkspace(): UseWorkspaceReturn {
    42→  const store = useWorkspaceState();
    43→  const [createWorkspaceName, setCreateWorkspaceName] = useState("");
    44→  const [createWorkspaceDescription, setCreateWorkspaceDescription] = useState("");
    45→  const [isCreatingWorkspace, setIsCreatingWorkspace] = useState(false);
    46→  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | undefined>();
    47→
    48→  const canCreateWorkspace = createWorkspaceName.trim().length > 0;
    49→
    50→  useEffect(() => {
    51→    const existingWorkspaceId = window.localStorage.getItem(ACTIVE_WORKSPACE_STORAGE_KEY);
    52→    if (!existingWorkspaceId) {
    53→      return;
    54→    }
    55→
    56→    void bootstrapWorkspace(existingWorkspaceId);
    57→  }, []);
    58→
    59→  async function bootstrapWorkspace(workspaceId: string) {
    60→    store.setIsBootstrapping(true);
    61→    setErrorMessage(undefined);
    62→
    63→    let response: Response;
    64→    try {
    65→      response = await fetch(`/api/workspaces/${encodeURIComponent(workspaceId)}/bootstrap`);
    66→    } catch (error) {
    67→      const messageText = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Network error";
    68→      setErrorMessage(messageText);
    69→      store.setIsBootstrapping(false);
    70→      return;
    71→    }
    72→
    73→    if (!response.ok) {
    74→      if (response.status === 404) {
    75→        window.localStorage.removeItem(ACTIVE_WORKSPACE_STORAGE_KEY);
    76→        store.clearWorkspace();
    77→      }
    78→      const body = await response.text();
    79→      setErrorMessage(body);
    80→      store.setIsBootstrapping(false);
    81→      return;
    82→    }
    83→
    84→    const payload = (await response.json()) as WorkspaceBootstrapResponse;
    85→    applyBootstrapPayload(payload);
    86→    store.setIsBootstrapping(false);
    87→  }
    88→
    89→  function applyBootstrapPayload(payload: WorkspaceBootstrapResponse) {
    90→    const parsed = parseBootstrapMessages(payload);
    91→
    92→    store.setSeedItems(payload.seeds);
    93→    store.setSidebar(payload.sidebar);
    94→    store.setBootstrapPayload(parsed);
    95→    store.applyWorkspace({
    96→      id: payload.workspaceId,
    97→      name: payload.workspaceName,
    98→      onboardingComplete: payload.onboardingComplete,
    99→      onboardingState: payload.onboardingState,
   100→      conversationId: payload.conversationId,
   101→    });
   102→  }
   103→
   104→  async function onCreateWorkspace(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
   105→    event.preventDefault();
   106→
   107→    const name = createWorkspaceName.trim();
   108→    if (isCreatingWorkspace) {
   109→      return;
   110→    }
   111→
   112→    if (!name) {
   113→      setErrorMessage("Workspace name is required");
   114→      return;
   115→    }
   116→
   117→    setErrorMessage(undefined);
   118→    setIsCreatingWorkspace(true);
   119→
   120→    const description = createWorkspaceDescription.trim();
   121→    const requestBody: CreateWorkspaceRequest = {
   122→      name,
   123→      ...(description.length > 0 ? { description } : {}),
   124→    };
   125→
   126→    try {
   127→      const response = await fetch("/api/workspaces", {
   128→        method: "POST",
   129→        headers: {
   130→          "Content-Type": "application/json",
   131→        },
   132→        body: JSON.stringify(requestBody),
   133→      });
   134→
   135→      if (!response.ok) {
   136→        const body = await response.text();
   137→        setErrorMessage(body);
   138→        return;
   139→      }
   140→
   141→      const payload = (await response.json()) as Partial<CreateWorkspaceResponse>;
   142→      if (typeof payload.workspaceId !== "string" || payload.workspaceId.trim().length === 0) {
   143→        throw new Error("create workspace response is missing workspaceId");
   144→      }
   145→
   146→      window.localStorage.setItem(ACTIVE_WORKSPACE_STORAGE_KEY, payload.workspaceId);
   147→      await bootstrapWorkspace(payload.workspaceId);
   148→      setCreateWorkspaceName("");
   149→      setCreateWorkspaceDescription("");
   150→    } catch (error) {
   151→      const messageText = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Workspace creation failed";
   152→      setErrorMessage(messageText);
   153→    } finally {
   154→      setIsCreatingWorkspace(false);
   155→    }
   156→  }
   157→
   158→  return {
   159→    isReady: store.workspaceId !== undefined && !store.isBootstrapping,
   160→    isBootstrapping: store.isBootstrapping,
   161→    isCreatingWorkspace,
   162→    errorMessage,
   163→    createWorkspaceName,
   164→    createWorkspaceDescription,
   165→    canCreateWorkspace,
   166→    setCreateWorkspaceName,
   167→    setCreateWorkspaceDescription,
   168→    onCreateWorkspace,
   169→  };
   170→}
   171→
```

     1→import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type KeyboardEvent } from "react";
     2→import { useMatch, useSearch } from "@tanstack/react-router";
     3→import Markdown from "react-markdown";
     4→import { ChatSuggestionPills } from "../components/chat/ChatSuggestionPills";
     5→import { DiscussEntityCard } from "../components/chat/DiscussEntityCard";
     6→import { EntityLink } from "../components/chat/EntityLink";
     7→import { SuggestionToolCard } from "../components/chat/SuggestionToolCard";
     8→import type { DiscussEntitySummary } from "../../shared/contracts";
     9→import { useWorkspaceState } from "../stores/workspace-state";
    10→import { useChatSession } from "../hooks/use-chat-session";
    11→import { useGovernanceFeed } from "../hooks/use-governance-feed";
    12→
    13→export function ChatPage() {
    14→  const onboardingState = useWorkspaceState((s) => s.onboardingState);
    15→  const setSidebarHandlers = useWorkspaceState((s) => s.setSidebarHandlers);
    16→
    17→  // Read conversationId from /chat/$conversationId route (undefined on /chat)
    18→  const matchWithId = useMatch({ from: "/authenticated/chat/$conversationId", shouldThrow: false });
    19→  const routeConversationId = matchWithId?.params.conversationId;
    20→
    21→  // Read message search param (works on both /chat and /chat/:id)
    22→  const search = useSearch({ strict: false });
    23→  const messageParam = (search as { message?: string })?.message;
    24→
    25→  const chat = useChatSession(routeConversationId);
    26→  const { feed } = useGovernanceFeed();
    27→
    28→  // Keep a ref to the latest chat handlers so the effect below doesn't
    29→  // depend on unstable function references.
    30→  const chatRef = useRef(chat);
    31→  chatRef.current = chat;
    32→
    33→  // Register sidebar handlers so the shell sidebar can interact with chat
    34→  useEffect(() => {
    35→    setSidebarHandlers({
    36→      activeConversationId: chat.activeConversationId,
    37→      isLoading: chat.isLoading,
    38→      onNewConversation: () => chatRef.current.newConversation(),
    39→      onSelectConversation: (id: string) => chatRef.current.selectConversation(id),
    40→    });
    41→  }, [chat.activeConversationId, chat.isLoading]);
    42→
    43→  // Cleanup sidebar handlers on unmount
    44→  useEffect(() => {
    45→    return () => {
    46→      setSidebarHandlers(undefined);
    47→    };
    48→  }, []);
    49→
    50→  // Load conversation from URL param on mount/param change
    51→  useEffect(() => {
    52→    if (routeConversationId && routeConversationId !== chat.activeConversationId) {
    53→      chat.selectConversation(routeConversationId);
    54→    } else if (!routeConversationId && chat.activeConversationId) {
    55→      // Navigated from /chat/:id to /chat (e.g., discuss on new chat)
    56→      chat.resetChat();
    57→    }
    58→  }, [routeConversationId]);
    59→
    60→  // Scroll to message from ?message= search param
    61→  useEffect(() => {
    62→    if (!messageParam) return;
    63→
    64→    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
    65→      const element = document.querySelector(`[data-message-id="${CSS.escape(messageParam)}"]`);
    66→      if (element) {
    67→        element.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "center" });
    68→        element.classList.add("animate-highlight");
    69→        element.addEventListener("animationend", () => {
    70→          element.classList.remove("animate-highlight");
    71→        }, { once: true });
    72→      }
    73→    }, 100);
    74→
    75→    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    76→  }, [messageParam, chat.messages]);
    77→
    78→  // Auto-scroll to bottom (skip when targeting a specific message)
    79→  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    80→  useEffect(() => {
    81→    if (messageParam) return;
    82→    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
    83→  }, [chat.messages]);
    84→
    85→  return (
    86→    <section className="chat-page">
    87→      {(chat.discussEntity ?? chat.conversationDiscussEntity) ? (
    88→        <DiscussEntityCard
    89→          entity={(chat.discussEntity ?? chat.conversationDiscussEntity) as DiscussEntitySummary}
    90→        />
    91→      ) : undefined}
    92→
    93→      <div className="chat-messages">
    94→        {chat.messages.length === 0
    95→          && onboardingState === "complete"
    96→          && !chat.discussEntity
    97→          && !chat.conversationDiscussEntity ? (
    98→          <ChatSuggestionPills feed={feed} />
    99→        ) : undefined}
   100→        {chat.messages.map((message) => (
   101→          <div
   102→            key={message.id}
   103→            data-message-id={message.id}
   104→            className={`chat-message chat-message--${message.role}`}
   105→          >
   106→            {message.role === "user" ? (
   107→              <div className="chat-message-label">You</div>
   108→            ) : undefined}
   109→            {message.parts.map((part, i) => {
   110→              if (part.type === "text") {
   111→                return (
   112→                  <div key={i} className="chat-message-text">
   113→                    <Markdown components={{ a: EntityLink }}>{part.text}</Markdown>
   114→                  </div>
   115→                );
   116→              }
   117→              if (part.type === "reasoning") {
   118→                return (
   119→                  <details key={i} className="thinking-block">
   120→                    <summary>Thinking</summary>
   121→                    <pre className="thinking-content">{part.text}</pre>
   122→                  </details>
   123→                );
   124→              }
   125→              if (isToolPart(part)) {
   126→                const toolPart = part as { type: string; state: string; output?: unknown };
   127→                const toolName = toolPart.type.replace(/^tool-/, "");
   128→
   129→                if (toolName === "create_suggestion" && toolPart.state === "output-available" && toolPart.output) {
   130→                  const output = toolPart.output as { text: string; category: string; confidence: number; rationale: string; target?: string };
   131→                  return <SuggestionToolCard key={i} output={output} />;
   132→                }
   133→
   134→                return (
   135→                  <div key={i} className="chat-tool-invocation">
   136→                    <span className="chat-tool-name">{toolName}</span>
   137→                    {toolPart.state === "output-available" ? (
   138→                      <span className="chat-tool-status">Done</span>
   139→                    ) : (
   140→                      <span className="chat-tool-status chat-tool-status--running">Running...</span>
   141→                    )}
   142→                  </div>
   143→                );
   144→              }
   145→              return undefined;
   146→            })}
   147→            {message.role === "assistant" ? (
   148→              <button
   149→                type="button"
   150→                className="branch-message-btn"
   151→                onClick={() => void chat.branchFromMessage(message.id)}
   152→                disabled={chat.isLoading || chat.branchingFromId !== undefined}
   153→                title="Branch from here"
   154→              >
   155→                <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true">
   156→                  <path d="M5 3v4.5c0 1.1.9 2 2 2h2.5M5 3L3 5M5 3l2 2M11 5v4.5c0 1.1-.9 2-2 2H6.5M11 5l-2-2M11 5l2-2" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.5" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round"/>
   157→                </svg>
   158→              </button>
   159→            ) : undefined}
   160→          </div>
   161→        ))}
   162→        <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
   163→      </div>
   164→
   165→      {onboardingState === "summary_pending" ? (
   166→        <div className="onboarding-action-buttons">
   167→          <button
   168→            type="button"
   169→            disabled={chat.isLoading}
   170→            onClick={() =>
   171→              chat.sendMessage("Looks good, let's go.", {
   172→                onboardingAction: "finalize_onboarding",
   173→              })}
   174→          >
   175→            Looks good, let's go
   176→          </button>
   177→          <button
   178→            type="button"
   179→            disabled={chat.isLoading}
   180→            onClick={() =>
   181→              chat.sendMessage("I want to add more.", {
   182→                onboardingAction: "continue_onboarding",
   183→              })}
   184→          >
   185→            I want to add more
   186→          </button>
   187→        </div>
   188→      ) : undefined}
   189→
   190→      <ChatInput
   191→        onSend={chat.sendMessage}
   192→        disabled={chat.isLoading}
   193→        onStop={chat.stopMessage}
   194→        isStreaming={chat.status === "streaming"}
   195→      />
   196→
   197→      {chat.errorMessage ? <p className="error-message">{chat.errorMessage}</p> : undefined}
   198→    </section>
   199→  );
   200→}
   201→
   202→function isToolPart(part: { type: string }): boolean {
   203→  return part.type.startsWith("tool-");
   204→}
   205→
   206→function ChatInput({
   207→  onSend,
   208→  disabled,
   209→  onStop,
   210→  isStreaming,
   211→}: {
   212→  onSend: (text: string) => void;
   213→  disabled: boolean;
   214→  onStop: () => void;
   215→  isStreaming: boolean;
   216→}) {
   217→  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
   218→  const textareaRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
   219→
   220→  function handleSubmit() {
   221→    const trimmed = input.trim();
   222→    if (!trimmed || disabled) return;
   223→    onSend(trimmed);
   224→    setInput("");
   225→    // Reset textarea height
   226→    if (textareaRef.current) {
   227→      textareaRef.current.style.height = "auto";
   228→    }
   229→  }
   230→
   231→  function handleKeyDown(e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) {
   232→    if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey) {
   233→      e.preventDefault();
   234→      handleSubmit();
   235→    }
   236→  }
   237→
   238→  // Auto-resize textarea
   239→  useEffect(() => {
   240→    const textarea = textareaRef.current;
   241→    if (!textarea) return;
   242→    textarea.style.height = "auto";
   243→    textarea.style.height = `${Math.min(textarea.scrollHeight, 200)}px`;
   244→  }, [input]);
   245→
   246→  return (
   247→    <div className="chat-input-container">
   248→      <textarea
   249→        ref={textareaRef}
   250→        className="chat-input"
   251→        value={input}
   252→        onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
   253→        onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
   254→        placeholder="Discuss tasks, decisions, and questions..."
   255→        rows={1}
   256→        disabled={disabled && !isStreaming}
   257→      />
   258→      {isStreaming ? (
   259→        <button
   260→          type="button"
   261→          className="chat-input-btn chat-input-btn--stop"
   262→          onClick={onStop}
   263→          title="Stop"
   264→        >
   265→          Stop
   266→        </button>
   267→      ) : (
   268→        <button
   269→          type="button"
   270→          className="chat-input-btn chat-input-btn--send"
   271→          onClick={handleSubmit}
   272→          disabled={disabled || input.trim().length === 0}
   273→          title="Send"
   274→        >
   275→          Send
   276→        </button>
   277→      )}
   278→    </div>
   279→  );
   280→}
   281→
```

Let me check if there's a timing issue — the processor sets `now = new Date()` at the START of processing (line 37), then uses it ~18 seconds later for the assistant message `createdAt`. If somehow this early `now` ends up equal to or before the user message `createdAt`, the order would be wrong.

     1→import type { ToolExecutionOptions } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     3→import type { AgentType, ChatToolExecutionContext } from "../chat/tools/types";
     4→import { requireToolContext } from "../chat/tools/helpers";
     5→import { jsonError } from "../http/response";
     6→
     7→export type AuthorityAction =
     8→  | "create_decision"
     9→  | "confirm_decision"
    10→  | "create_task"
    11→  | "complete_task"
    12→  | "create_observation"
    13→  | "acknowledge_observation"
    14→  | "resolve_observation"
    15→  | "create_question"
    16→  | "create_suggestion";
    17→
    18→export type AuthorityPermission = "auto" | "provisional" | "propose" | "blocked";
    19→
    20→export type AuthorizedContext = {
    21→  context: ChatToolExecutionContext;
    22→  permission: AuthorityPermission;
    23→};
    24→
    25→export class AuthorityError extends Error {
    26→  constructor(
    27→    public readonly permission: "propose" | "blocked",
    28→    public readonly action: AuthorityAction,
    29→    public readonly agentType: AgentType,
    30→  ) {
    31→    const verb = permission === "blocked" ? "not authorized" : "can only propose";
    32→    super(`${agentType} is ${verb} for action: ${action}`);
    33→    this.name = "AuthorityError";
    34→  }
    35→}
    36→
    37→const ACTOR_DEFAULT_AGENT_TYPE: Partial<Record<string, AgentType>> = {
    38→  pm_agent: "management",
    39→  analytics_agent: "observer",
    40→};
    41→
    42→export async function checkAuthority(input: {
    43→  surreal: Surreal;
    44→  agentType: AgentType;
    45→  action: AuthorityAction;
    46→  workspaceRecord?: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    47→}): Promise<AuthorityPermission> {
    48→  // 1. Try workspace-specific override
    49→  if (input.workspaceRecord) {
    50→    const [wsRows] = await input.surreal.query<[Array<{ permission: string }>]>(
    51→      "SELECT permission FROM authority_scope WHERE agent_type = $agentType AND action = $action AND workspace = $workspace LIMIT 1;",
    52→      {
    53→        agentType: input.agentType,
    54→        action: input.action,
    55→        workspace: input.workspaceRecord,
    56→      },
    57→    );
    58→
    59→    if (wsRows.length > 0) {
    60→      return wsRows[0].permission as AuthorityPermission;
    61→    }
    62→  }
    63→
    64→  // 2. Fall back to global default (workspace IS NONE)
    65→  const [globalRows] = await input.surreal.query<[Array<{ permission: string }>]>(
    66→    "SELECT permission FROM authority_scope WHERE agent_type = $agentType AND action = $action AND workspace IS NONE LIMIT 1;",
    67→    { agentType: input.agentType, action: input.action },
    68→  );
    69→
    70→  if (globalRows.length > 0) {
    71→    return globalRows[0].permission as AuthorityPermission;
    72→  }
    73→
    74→  // 3. Fail-safe: no row = blocked
    75→  return "blocked";
    76→}
    77→
    78→export async function requireAuthorizedContext(
    79→  options: ToolExecutionOptions,
    80→  action: AuthorityAction,
    81→  deps: { surreal: Surreal },
    82→): Promise<AuthorizedContext> {
    83→  const context = requireToolContext(options);
    84→
    85→  // Contract: MCP contexts must never claim human presence
    86→  if (context.humanPresent && context.actor === "mcp") {
    87→    throw new Error("Contract violation: humanPresent must not be true for MCP contexts");
    88→  }
    89→
    90→  // Human-present web sessions bypass authority (human IS the authority)
    91→  if (context.humanPresent) {
    92→    return { context, permission: "auto" };
    93→  }
    94→
    95→  const agentType: AgentType =
    96→    context.agentType ?? ACTOR_DEFAULT_AGENT_TYPE[context.actor] ?? "code_agent";
    97→
    98→  const permission = await checkAuthority({
    99→    surreal: deps.surreal,
   100→    agentType,
   101→    action,
   102→    workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   103→  });
   104→
   105→  if (permission === "blocked") {
   106→    throw new AuthorityError("blocked", action, agentType);
   107→  }
   108→
   109→  if (permission === "propose") {
   110→    throw new AuthorityError("propose", action, agentType);
   111→  }
   112→
   113→  // "auto" or "provisional" — caller proceeds (may mark output as provisional)
   114→  return { context, permission };
   115→}
   116→
   117→export function checkAuthorityOrError(
   118→  permission: AuthorityPermission,
   119→  action: AuthorityAction,
   120→  agentType: AgentType,
   121→): Response | undefined {
   122→  if (permission === "blocked") {
   123→    return jsonError(`${agentType} is not authorized for action: ${action}`, 403);
   124→  }
   125→  if (permission === "propose") {
   126→    return jsonError(
   127→      `${agentType} can only propose ${action} — requires human approval`,
   128→      403,
   129→    );
   130→  }
   131→  return undefined;
   132→}
   133→
```

    37→const ACTOR_DEFAULT_AGENT_TYPE: Partial<Record<string, AgentType>> = {
    38→  pm_agent: "management",
    39→  analytics_agent: "observer",
    40→};
    41→
    42→export async function checkAuthority(input: {
    43→  surreal: Surreal;
    44→  agentType: AgentType;
    45→  action: AuthorityAction;
    46→  workspaceRecord?: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    47→}): Promise<AuthorityPermission> {
    48→  // 1. Try workspace-specific override
    49→  if (input.workspaceRecord) {
    50→    const [wsRows] = await input.surreal.query<[Array<{ permission: string }>]>(
    51→      "SELECT permission FROM authority_scope WHERE agent_type = $agentType AND action = $action AND workspace = $workspace LIMIT 1;",
    52→      {
    53→        agentType: input.agentType,
    54→        action: input.action,
    55→        workspace: input.workspaceRecord,
    56→      },
    57→    );
    58→
    59→    if (wsRows.length > 0) {
    60→      return wsRows[0].permission as AuthorityPermission;
    61→    }
    62→  }
    63→
    64→  // 2. Fall back to global default (workspace IS NONE)
    65→  const [globalRows] = await input.surreal.query<[Array<{ permission: string }>]>(
    66→    "SELECT permission FROM authority_scope WHERE agent_type = $agentType AND action = $action AND workspace IS NONE LIMIT 1;",
    67→    { agentType: input.agentType, action: input.action },
    68→  );
    69→
    70→  if (globalRows.length > 0) {
    71→    return globalRows[0].permission as AuthorityPermission;
    72→  }
    73→
    74→  // 3. Fail-safe: no row = blocked
    75→  return "blocked";
    76→}
    77→
    78→export async function requireAuthorizedContext(
    79→  options: ToolExecutionOptions,
    80→  action: AuthorityAction,
    81→  deps: { surreal: Surreal },
    82→): Promise<AuthorizedContext> {
    83→  const context = requireToolContext(options);
    84→
    85→  // Contract: MCP contexts must never claim human presence
    86→  if (context.humanPresent && context.actor === "mcp") {
    87→    throw new Error("Contract violation: humanPresent must not be true for MCP contexts");
    88→  }
    89→
    90→  // Human-present web sessions bypass authority (human IS the authority)
    91→  if (context.humanPresent) {
    92→    return { context, permission: "auto" };
    93→  }
    94→
    95→  const agentType: AgentType =
    96→    context.agentType ?? ACTOR_DEFAULT_AGENT_TYPE[context.actor] ?? "code_agent";
    97→
    98→  const permission = await checkAuthority({
    99→    surreal: deps.surreal,
   100→    agentType,
   101→    action,
   102→    workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   103→  });
   104→
   105→  if (permission === "blocked") {
   106→    throw new AuthorityError("blocked", action, agentType);
   107→  }
   108→
   109→  if (permission === "propose") {
   110→    throw new AuthorityError("propose", action, agentType);
   111→  }
   112→
   113→  // "auto" or "provisional" — caller proceeds (may mark output as provisional)
   114→  return { context, permission };
   115→}
   116→
   117→export function checkAuthorityOrError(
   118→  permission: AuthorityPermission,
   119→  action: AuthorityAction,
   120→  agentType: AgentType,
   121→): Response | undefined {
   122→  if (permission === "blocked") {
   123→    return jsonError(`${agentType} is not authorized for action: ${action}`, 403);
   124→  }
   125→  if (permission === "propose") {
   126→    return jsonError(
   127→      `${agentType} can only propose ${action} — requires human approval`,
   128→      403,
   129→    );
   130→  }
   131→  return undefined;
   132→}
   133→

     1→import type { ToolExecutionOptions } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     3→import type { ChatToolExecutionContext } from "./types";
     4→
     5→export function requireToolContext(options: ToolExecutionOptions): ChatToolExecutionContext {
     6→  const context = options.experimental_context as ChatToolExecutionContext | undefined;
     7→  if (!context) {
     8→    throw new Error("chat tool execution context is missing");
     9→  }
    10→
    11→  if (!context.workspaceRecord || !context.currentMessageRecord || !context.conversationRecord) {
    12→    throw new Error("chat tool execution context is malformed");
    13→  }
    14→
    15→  return context;
    16→}
    17→
    18→export function toDecisionRecordId(value: string): RecordId<"decision", string> {
    19→  const normalized = value.trim();
    20→  if (normalized.length === 0) {
    21→    throw new Error("decision id is required");
    22→  }
    23→
    24→  if (normalized.includes(":")) {
    25→    const [table, id] = normalized.split(":", 2);
    26→    if (table !== "decision" || !id) {
    27→      throw new Error("decision id must reference a decision record");
    28→    }
    29→    return new RecordId("decision", id);
    30→  }
    31→
    32→  return new RecordId("decision", normalized);
    33→}
    34→

    30→  embeddingDimension: number;
    31→  extractionModelId: string;
    32→  extractionModel: any;
    33→  extractionStoreThreshold: number;
    34→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
    35→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    36→  currentMessageRecord: RecordId<"message", string>;
    37→  latestUserText: string;
    38→  workspaceOwnerRecord?: RecordId<"person", string>;
    39→  personRecord: RecordId<"person", string>;
    40→  userMessageEmbedding?: number[];
    41→  inheritedEntityIds?: RecordId[];
    42→  discussesRecord?: RecordId;
    43→  messages: ConversationMessage[];
    44→  isOnboarding?: boolean;
    45→  onboardingState?: OnboardingState;
    46→  workspaceName: string;
    47→  workspaceDescription?: string;
    48→  onToken: (token: string) => Promise<void> | void;
    49→  onReasoning?: (token: string) => Promise<void> | void;
    50→}): Promise<ChatAgentResult> {
    51→  const context = await buildChatContext({
    52→    surreal: input.surreal,
    53→    conversationRecord: input.conversationRecord,
    54→    workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    55→    ...(input.workspaceDescription ? { workspaceDescription: input.workspaceDescription } : {}),
    56→    ...(input.userMessageEmbedding ? { userMessageEmbedding: input.userMessageEmbedding } : {}),
    57→    ...(input.inheritedEntityIds && input.inheritedEntityIds.length > 0
    58→      ? { inheritedEntityIds: input.inheritedEntityIds }
    59→      : {}),
    60→    ...(input.discussesRecord ? { discussesRecord: input.discussesRecord } : {}),
    61→  });
    62→
    63→  const system = buildSystemPrompt(context, {
    64→    isOnboarding: input.isOnboarding,
    65→    onboardingState: input.onboardingState,
    66→    workspaceName: input.workspaceName,
    67→  });
    68→
    69→  const modelMessages: ModelMessage[] = input.messages.map((message) => ({
    70→    role: message.role,
    71→    content: message.text,
    72→  }));
    73→
    74→  const result = streamText({
    75→    model: input.model,
    76→    system,
    77→    messages: modelMessages,
    78→    tools: createChatAgentTools({
    79→      surreal: input.surreal,
    80→      pmAgentModel: input.pmAgentModel,
    81→      analyticsAgentModel: input.analyticsAgentModel,
    82→      analyticsSurreal: input.analyticsSurreal,
    83→      embeddingModel: input.embeddingModel,
    84→      embeddingDimension: input.embeddingDimension,
    85→      extractionModelId: input.extractionModelId,
    86→      extractionModel: input.extractionModel,
    87→      extractionStoreThreshold: input.extractionStoreThreshold,
    88→    }),
    89→    experimental_context: {
    90→      actor: "chat_agent",
    91→      humanPresent: true,
    92→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    93→      conversationRecord: input.conversationRecord,
    94→      currentMessageRecord: input.currentMessageRecord,
    95→      latestUserText: input.latestUserText,
    96→      ...(input.workspaceOwnerRecord ? { workspaceOwnerRecord: input.workspaceOwnerRecord } : {}),
    97→      personRecord: input.personRecord,
    98→    },
    99→    stopWhen: stepCountIs(5),
   100→  });
   101→
   102→  let text = "";
   103→  const collectedEntities: CollectedEntity[] = [];
   104→  const collectedRelationships: CollectedRelationship[] = [];
   105→  const toolCalls: Array<{ name: string; args: Record<string, unknown> }> = [];
   106→
   107→  for await (const part of result.fullStream) {
   108→    if (part.type === "reasoning-delta") {
   109→      await input.onReasoning?.(part.text);
   110→      continue;
   111→    }
   112→
   113→    if (part.type === "text-delta") {
   114→      text = `${text}${part.text}`;
   115→      await input.onToken(part.text);
   116→      continue;
   117→    }
   118→
   119→    if (part.type === "tool-call") {
   120→      logInfo("chat.agent.tool_call", "Chat agent invoked tool", part);
   121→      toolCalls.push({ name: part.toolName, args: (part as any).input as Record<string, unknown> });
   122→    }
   123→
   124→    if (part.type === "tool-result") {
   125→      logInfo("chat.agent.tool_result", "Chat agent tool returned", part);
   126→      const toolResult = part.output as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   127→      if (toolResult) {
   128→        if (Array.isArray(toolResult.extracted_entities)) {
   129→          collectedEntities.push(...(toolResult.extracted_entities as CollectedEntity[]));
   130→        }
   131→        if (Array.isArray(toolResult.extracted_relationships)) {
   132→          collectedRelationships.push(...(toolResult.extracted_relationships as CollectedRelationship[]));
   133→        }
   134→      }
   135→    }
   136→
   137→    if (part.type === "error") {
   138→      logError("chat.agent.stream_error", "Chat agent stream error", part.error, {});
   139→    }
   140→  }
   141→
   142→  if (text.trim().length === 0) {
   143→    text = await result.text;
   144→  }
   145→
   146→  // Post-process: inject entity:// links for entity names mentioned in the response.
   147→  const linked = injectEntityLinks(text, context);
   148→  if (linked !== text) {
   149→    // Re-send the full linked text as a replacement.
   150→    // The SSE stream already sent raw text; the persisted version will have links.
   151→    text = linked;
   152→  }
   153→
   154→  return {
   155→    text: text.trim(),
   156→    collectedEntities,
   157→    collectedRelationships,
   158→    toolCalls,
   159→  };
   160→}
   161→
   162→export const runGraphAwareChat = runChatAgent;
   163→
   164→type KnownEntity = { entityId: string; kind: string; name: string };
   165→
   166→/**
   167→ * Replace entity name mentions in response text with markdown links:
   168→ *   "Model for Extraction Pipeline" → "[Model for Extraction Pipeline](entity://decision:uuid)"
   169→ *
   170→ * Matches longest names first to avoid partial replacement.
   171→ * Only replaces the first occurrence of each entity name.
   172→ */
   173→function injectEntityLinks(text: string, context: ChatContext): string {
   174→  const entities: KnownEntity[] = [];
   175→
   176→  for (const project of context.workspaceSummary.projects) {
   177→    entities.push({ entityId: `project:${project.id}`, kind: "project", name: project.name });
   178→  }
   179→  for (const decision of context.workspaceSummary.recentDecisions) {
   180→    entities.push({ entityId: `decision:${decision.id}`, kind: "decision", name: decision.name });
   181→  }
   182→  for (const question of context.workspaceSummary.openQuestions) {
   183→    entities.push({ entityId: `question:${question.id}`, kind: "question", name: question.name });
   184→  }
   185→
   186→  // Sort longest name first so "Agent-Native Business OS" matches before "Agent"
   187→  entities.sort((a, b) => b.name.length - a.name.length);
   188→
   189→  const replaced = new Set<string>();
   190→  let result = text;
   191→
   192→  for (const entity of entities) {
   193→    if (entity.name.length < 3) continue;
   194→    if (replaced.has(entity.entityId)) continue;
   195→
   196→    // Case-insensitive match, but only replace the first occurrence.
   197→    // Skip if the name is already inside a markdown link.
   198→    const idx = result.toLowerCase().indexOf(entity.name.toLowerCase());
   199→    if (idx === -1) continue;
   200→
   201→    // Check if already inside a markdown link: [...](...)
   202→    const before = result.slice(0, idx);
   203→    if (before.lastIndexOf("[") > before.lastIndexOf("]")) continue;
   204→
   205→    const matched = result.slice(idx, idx + entity.name.length);
   206→    const link = `[${matched}](#entity/${entity.entityId})`;
   207→    result = `${result.slice(0, idx)}${link}${result.slice(idx + entity.name.length)}`;
   208→    replaced.add(entity.entityId);
   209→  }
   210→
   211→  return result;
   212→}
   213→

1. **`agent.ts:85`** — PM agent now explicitly preserves `humanPresent` from chat context with `?? true` fallback, ensuring authority bypass works even if the SDK doesn't propagate all spread fields.

2. **`prompt.ts:78`** — Workspace name guard now instructs the PM agent to strip the workspace name from project names (e.g. "DabDash Dashboard" → "Dashboard") instead of just preventing exact matches.

You should also run `bun migrate` to ensure the `authority_scope` rows exist in the database — that's the fallback if `humanPresent` doesn't bypass.
